          {mode === 'srs' && (
            <div className="w-full max-w-xl flex flex-col items-center gap-5 animate-fade-in">
              <div className="w-full flex justify-between items-center text-xs text-gray-400 px-2 pb-1 border-b border-white/10">
                <span className="font-semibold text-indigo-400 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <BrainCircuit size={14} /> Ôn tập theo thuật toán SuperMemo-2 (SM-2)
                </span>
                <span className="font-mono">Thẻ {currentIndex + 1} / {cards.length}</span>
              </div>

              {/* Flashcard Card */}
              <div 
                onClick={() => setFlipped(!flipped)}
                className="w-full h-64 cursor-pointer rounded-3xl border border-indigo-500/30 bg-gradient-to-br from-indigo-950/40 to-black/60 p-7 flex flex-col justify-between shadow-xl"
              >
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-xs">
                  <span className="text-indigo-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">
                    {flipped ? "Định nghĩa / Giải thích" : "Khái niệm cần nhớ"}
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-gray-500 text-[11px] font-mono">
                    {srsInfo ? `Đã ôn ${srsInfo.repetitions} lần • Khoảng cách: ${srsInfo.interval_days} ngày` : "Thẻ mới"}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="text-lg md:text-xl font-bold text-center text-white my-auto leading-relaxed">
                  {flipped ? currentCard?.back : currentCard?.front}
                </div>
                <div className="text-center text-[11px] text-gray-500">
                  {flipped ? "Bấm vào các nút bên dưới để ghi nhận tiến độ" : "Nhấn để lật xem đáp án"}
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* 4 SRS Rating Buttons */}
              <div className="w-full grid grid-cols-4 gap-2 pt-2">
                <button
                  onClick={() => handleSrsReview(1)}
                  disabled={srsLoading}
                  className="p-3 rounded-2xl bg-red-500/15 hover:bg-red-500/25 border border-red-500/30 text-red-300 text-xs font-bold transition flex flex-col items-center gap-1 shadow-sm"
                >
                  <span>Lại (Again)</span>
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-normal text-red-400 font-mono">1 phút</span>
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={() => handleSrsReview(2)}
                  disabled={srsLoading}
                  className="p-3 rounded-2xl bg-orange-500/15 hover:bg-orange-500/25 border border-orange-500/30 text-orange-300 text-xs font-bold transition flex flex-col items-center gap-1 shadow-sm"
                >
                  <span>Khó (Hard)</span>
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-normal text-orange-400 font-mono">1 ngày</span>
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={() => handleSrsReview(3)}
                  disabled={srsLoading}
                  className="p-3 rounded-2xl bg-blue-500/15 hover:bg-blue-500/25 border border-blue-500/30 text-blue-300 text-xs font-bold transition flex flex-col items-center gap-1 shadow-sm"
                >
                  <span>Tốt (Good)</span>
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-normal text-blue-400 font-mono">3 ngày</span>
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={() => handleSrsReview(4)}
                  disabled={srsLoading}
                  className="p-3 rounded-2xl bg-emerald-500/15 hover:bg-emerald-500/25 border border-emerald-500/30 text-emerald-300 text-xs font-bold transition flex flex-col items-center gap-1 shadow-sm"
                >
                  <span>Dễ (Easy)</span>
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-normal text-emerald-400 font-mono">7 ngày</span>
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* MODE 3: PRACTICE MODE WITH TIMER & STREAKS */}
          {mode === 'practice' && (
            <div className="w-full max-w-xl animate-fade-in flex flex-col items-center">
              {practiceFinished ? (
                <div className="text-center py-8 space-y-4">
                  <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-400 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto shadow-xl">
                    <Trophy size={32} />
                  </div>
                  <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white">Hoàn thành bài thi thử!</h3>
                  <div className="flex justify-center gap-6 text-sm font-mono pt-2">
                    <div className="p-3 rounded-2xl bg-white/5 border border-white/10">
                      <div className="text-gray-400 text-xs">Tổng điểm</div>
                      <div className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-amber-400">{practiceScore}</div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="p-3 rounded-2xl bg-white/5 border border-white/10">
                      <div className="text-gray-400 text-xs">Chuỗi đúng nhất</div>
                      <div className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-orange-400">{practiceMaxStreak} 🔥</div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => startPractice(practiceDuration)}
                    className="bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-400 text-black font-bold text-xs px-6 py-2.5 rounded-full transition shadow-lg mt-4"
                  >
                    Thi lại lần nữa
                  </button>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="w-full space-y-4">
                  {/* Timer bar & Stats */}
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-mono font-bold">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <Timer size={16} className={practiceTimeLeft < 15 ? "text-red-400 animate-pulse" : "text-amber-400"} />
                      <span className={practiceTimeLeft < 15 ? "text-red-400" : "text-amber-300"}>
                        {practiceTimeLeft}s
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-orange-400">
                      <Flame size={16} className={practiceStreak > 2 ? "animate-bounce" : ""} />
                      <span>Streak: {practiceStreak} 🔥</span>
                    </div>

                    <div className="text-green-400">
                      Điểm: {practiceScore}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Progress Line */}
                  <div className="w-full h-1.5 bg-white/10 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                    <div 
                      className={`h-full transition-all duration-1000 ${practiceTimeLeft < 15 ? 'bg-red-500' : 'bg-amber-500'}`}
                      style={{ width: `${(practiceTimeLeft / practiceDuration) * 100}%` }}
                    />
                  </div>

                  {/* Flashcard in practice mode */}
                  <div 
                    onClick={() => setFlipped(!flipped)}
                    className="w-full h-60 rounded-3xl border border-amber-500/30 bg-gradient-to-br from-amber-950/20 to-black/70 p-6 flex flex-col justify-between cursor-pointer shadow-xl select-none"
                  >
                    <div className="flex justify-between text-xs text-amber-400 font-bold uppercase">
                      <span>{flipped ? "Đáp án:" : "Câu hỏi / Khái niệm:"}</span>
                      <span className="text-gray-400 text-[11px] font-normal">Nhấn để lật</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-lg md:text-xl font-bold text-center text-white my-auto leading-relaxed">
                      {flipped ? currentCard?.back : currentCard?.front}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-center text-[11px] text-gray-500">
                      Thẻ {currentIndex + 1} / {cards.length}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Yes / No Answer Buttons */}
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 pt-2">
                    <button
                      onClick={() => handlePracticeAnswer(false)}
                      className="py-3 rounded-2xl bg-red-500/20 hover:bg-red-500/30 border border-red-500/40 text-red-300 font-bold text-xs transition"
                    >
                      ✕ Trả lời sai (0đ)
                    </button>
                    <button
                      onClick={() => handlePracticeAnswer(true)}
                      className="py-3 rounded-2xl bg-green-500/20 hover:bg-green-500/30 border border-green-500/40 text-green-300 font-bold text-xs transition"
                    >
                      ✓ Trả lời đúng (+{10 * (1 + Math.floor(practiceStreak / 3))}đ)
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* MODE 4: MATCH GAME */}
          {mode === 'match' && (
            <div className="w-full max-w-2xl flex flex-col items-center animate-fade-in">
              <div className="w-full flex justify-between items-center text-xs font-mono mb-4 text-gray-400">
                <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-blue-400 font-semibold">
                  <Timer size={14} /> Thời gian: {gameTimer}s
                </span>
                <span>Đã ghép: {matchedIds.size} / {Math.min(cards.length, 6)}</span>
              </div>

              {!gameFinished ? (
                <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3 w-full">
                  {matchTiles.map(tile => {
                    const isMatched = matchedIds.has(tile.pairId);
                    const isSelected = selectedTile?.id === tile.id;
                    const isWrong = wrongTileId === tile.id;

                    if (isMatched) {
                      return (
                        <div key={tile.id} className="h-24 rounded-2xl border border-green-500/20 bg-green-500/5 opacity-30 flex items-center justify-center p-3 text-center text-xs text-green-300 font-medium">
                          ✓ Đã ghép đúng
                        </div>
                      );
                    }

                    return (
                      <div
                        key={tile.id}
                        onClick={() => handleTileClick(tile)}
                        className={`h-24 rounded-2xl p-3 flex items-center justify-center text-center text-xs font-semibold cursor-pointer select-none transition-all duration-200 border ${
                          isWrong 
                            ? 'bg-red-500/30 border-red-500 text-red-200 animate-shake'
                            : isSelected
                            ? 'bg-blue-600/30 border-blue-500 text-white scale-105 shadow-lg'
                            : 'bg-white/5 border-white/10 hover:border-white/25 text-gray-300 hover:text-white'
                        }`}
                      >
                        <span className="line-clamp-3">{tile.text}</span>
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="text-center py-10 space-y-4 animate-fade-in">
                  <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-yellow-500/20 text-yellow-400 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto shadow-xl">
                    <Trophy size={32} />
                  </div>
                  <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white">Xuất sắc! Bạn đã ghép xong</h3>
                  <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm">
                    Thời gian hoàn thành: <span className="text-yellow-400 font-bold font-mono text-base">{gameTimer} giây</span>
                  </p>
                  <button
                    onClick={startMatchGame}
                    className="bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white font-semibold text-xs px-6 py-2.5 rounded-full transition shadow-lg shadow-blue-600/30"
                  >
                    Chơi lại để phá kỷ lục
                  </button>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* MODE 5: EXPORT TO QUIZLET.COM */}
          {mode === 'export' && (
            <div className="w-full max-w-xl space-y-5 animate-fade-in">
              <div className="bg-purple-500/10 border border-purple-500/20 p-4 rounded-2xl text-xs text-purple-200 space-y-2">
                <h4 className="font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 text-purple-300">
                  <ExternalLink size={14} /> Cách nạp nhanh vào Quizlet.com:
                </h4>
                <ol className="list-decimal list-inside space-y-1 text-gray-300">
                  <li>Bấm nút <strong>"Sao chép toàn bộ thẻ"</strong> bên dưới.</li>
                  <li>Truy cập <a href="https://quizlet.com/create-set" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" className="text-purple-400 underline font-semibold">quizlet.com/create-set</a></li>
                  <li>Bấm vào <strong>"Nhập từ Word, Excel, Google Docs"</strong> và dán (Ctrl+V / Cmd+V) vào ô. Xong!</li>
                </ol>
              </div>

              <div>
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-1.5">
                  <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-400">Dữ liệu định dạng Tab-Separated:</label>
                  <button
                    onClick={handleCopyExport}
                    className="text-xs px-3 py-1 rounded-lg bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/20 text-white flex items-center gap-1 transition"
                  >
                    {copied ? <CheckCheck size={14} className="text-green-400" /> : <Copy size={14} />}
                    <span>{copied ? "Đã sao chép!" : "Sao chép toàn bộ"}</span>
                  </button>
                </div>
                <textarea
                  readOnly
                  value={cards.map(c => `${c.front}\t${c.back}`).join('\n')}
                  rows={8}
                  className="w-full bg-black/40 border border-white/15 rounded-xl p-3 font-mono text-xs text-gray-300 focus:outline-none select-all"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="flex justify-end">
                <a
                  href="https://quizlet.com/create-set"
                  target="_blank"
                  rel="noreferrer"
                  className="bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600 to-purple-600 hover:opacity-90 text-white font-semibold text-xs px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl transition flex items-center gap-2 shadow-lg"
                >
                  Mở trang tạo học phần Quizlet.com <ExternalLink size={14} />
                </a>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

        </div>

        {/* Footer */}
        <div className={`p-4 border-t flex items-center justify-between text-xs ${
          isDark ? 'border-white/10 bg-white/[0.01] text-gray-500' : 'border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 text-gray-500'
        }`}>
          <span>Hỗ trợ ôn luyện ghi nhớ ngắt quãng Spaced Repetition & Ghép thẻ tương tác.</span>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="px-4 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/20 text-gray-200 transition font-medium">
            Đóng
          </button>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


# ==============================================================================
# FILE: frontend/src/components/SettingsModal.jsx
# ==============================================================================

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { X, Check, Server, Key, Cpu, Cloud, RefreshCw, AlertCircle, Eye, EyeOff, ExternalLink } from 'lucide-react';
import axios from 'axios';

export default function SettingsModal({ isOpen, onClose, onSettingsSaved }) {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('local'); // 'local' or 'cloud'
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [scanning, setScanning] = useState(false);
  const [localStatus, setLocalStatus] = useState({ ollama: { available: false, models: [] }, lm_studio: { available: false, models: [] } });
  
  // Settings State
  const [settings, setSettings] = useState({
    default_provider: 'ollama',
    default_model: 'qwen2.5:3b',
    ollama_base_url: 'http://localhost:11434',
    lm_studio_base_url: 'http://localhost:1234/v1',
    gemini_api_key: '',
    gemini_model: 'gemini-2.5-flash',
    openai_api_key: '',
    openai_model: 'gpt-4o-mini',
    groq_api_key: '',
    groq_model: 'llama-3.3-70b-versatile',
    openrouter_api_key: '',
    openrouter_model: 'deepseek/deepseek-r1',
    router9_base_url: 'http://localhost:20128/v1',
    router9_api_key: '',
    router9_model: 'claude-3-5-sonnet',
    custom_base_url: '',
    custom_api_key: '',
    custom_model: ''
  });

  const [testResult, setTestResult] = useState({});
  const [testingProvider, setTestingProvider] = useState(null);
  const [showKeys, setShowKeys] = useState({});

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      loadSettings();
      scanLocalModels();
    }
  }, [isOpen]);

  const loadSettings = async () => {
    try {
      const res = await axios.get('/api/settings');
      if (res.data) {
        setSettings(prev => ({ ...prev, ...res.data }));
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("Lỗi tải cài đặt:", e);
    }
  };

  const scanLocalModels = async () => {
    setScanning(true);
    try {
      const res = await axios.get('/api/llm/local-models');
      setLocalStatus(res.data);
      // Nếu có model ollama mà default_model rỗng, chọn model đầu tiên
      if (res.data?.ollama?.models?.length > 0 && (!settings.default_model || settings.default_model === 'qwen2.5:3b')) {
        const found = res.data.ollama.models.find(m => m.name === settings.default_model);
        if (!found) {
          setSettings(prev => ({ ...prev, default_model: res.data.ollama.models[0].name }));
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("Lỗi quét local models:", e);
    } finally {
      setScanning(false);
    }
  };

  const handleTestConnection = async (provider, modelName, apiKey, baseUrl) => {
    setTestingProvider(provider);
    setTestResult(prev => ({ ...prev, [provider]: { testing: true } }));
    try {
      const res = await axios.post('/api/llm/test-connection', {
        provider,
        model_name: modelName,
        api_key: apiKey,
        base_url: baseUrl
      });
      setTestResult(prev => ({ ...prev, [provider]: res.data }));
    } catch (e) {
      setTestResult(prev => ({
        ...prev,
        [provider]: { success: false, message: e.response?.data?.detail || e.message }
      }));
    } finally {
      setTestingProvider(null);
    }
  };

  const handleSave = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await axios.post('/api/settings', settings);
      if (onSettingsSaved) onSettingsSaved(settings);
      onClose();
    } catch (e) {
      alert("Lỗi lưu cài đặt: " + (e.response?.data?.detail || e.message));
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const toggleShowKey = (field) => {
    setShowKeys(prev => ({ ...prev, [field]: !prev[field] }));
  };

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/75 backdrop-blur-sm p-4 animate-fade-in font-sans">
      <div className="bg-[#181a20] border border-white/10 rounded-2xl w-full max-w-2xl max-h-[90vh] flex flex-col shadow-2xl overflow-hidden">
        
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="p-5 border-b border-white/10 flex items-center justify-between bg-white/[0.02]">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-blue-500/10 border border-blue-500/20 flex items-center justify-center text-blue-400">
              <Cpu size={22} />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-white">Cấu hình Bộ Não AI</h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">Cắm bất kỳ Local AI hoặc Cloud API nào một cách dễ dàng</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="p-2 text-gray-400 hover:text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-white/5 transition">
            <X size={20} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Tabs */}
        <div className="flex border-b border-white/10 px-5 bg-white/[0.01]">
          <button
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('local')}
            className={`py-3 px-4 text-xs font-semibold border-b-2 flex items-center gap-2 transition-all ${
              activeTab === 'local'
                ? 'border-green-500 text-green-400'
                : 'border-transparent text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-200'
            }`}
          >
            <Server size={15} /> Local AI (Ollama / Trên máy)
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('9router')}
            className={`py-3 px-4 text-xs font-semibold border-b-2 flex items-center gap-2 transition-all ${
              activeTab === '9router'
                ? 'border-purple-500 text-purple-400'
                : 'border-transparent text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-200'
            }`}
          >
            <Cpu size={15} /> 9Router Gateway
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('cloud')}
            className={`py-3 px-4 text-xs font-semibold border-b-2 flex items-center gap-2 transition-all ${
              activeTab === 'cloud'
                ? 'border-blue-500 text-blue-400'
                : 'border-transparent text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-200'
            }`}
          >
            <Cloud size={15} /> Cloud AI (API Key)
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Tab Content */}
        <div className="p-6 overflow-y-auto flex-1 space-y-6 scrollbar-hide">
          
          {/* TAB 1: LOCAL AI */}
          {activeTab === 'local' && (
            <div className="space-y-6 animate-fade-in">
              {/* Ollama Section */}
              <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/10 space-y-4">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="font-semibold text-white">Ollama</span>
                    {localStatus?.ollama?.available ? (
                      <span className="text-xs px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-green-500/20 text-green-400 border border-green-500/30 flex items-center gap-1">
                        <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-green-400 animate-pulse"></span> Đã kết nối ({localStatus.ollama.models.length} model)
                      </span>
                    ) : (
                      <span className="text-xs px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-red-500/20 text-red-400 border border-red-500/30">
                        Chưa tìm thấy
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <button 
                    onClick={scanLocalModels} 
                    disabled={scanning}
                    className="text-xs flex items-center gap-1.5 text-gray-400 hover:text-white bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-white/10 transition"
                  >
                    <RefreshCw size={13} className={scanning ? 'animate-spin' : ''} /> Quét lại
                  </button>
                </div>

                {localStatus?.ollama?.available ? (
                  <div className="space-y-3">
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-gray-400 mb-1.5">Chọn Model Ollama mặc định:</label>
                      <select
                        value={settings.default_model}
                        onChange={e => setSettings({ ...settings, default_model: e.target.value })}
                        className="w-full bg-[#121316] border border-white/15 rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-green-500"
                      >
                        {localStatus.ollama.models.map(m => (
                          <option key={m.name} value={m.name}>
                            {m.name} ({m.size_gb} GB - {m.details?.parameter_size || 'Local'})
                          </option>
                        ))}
                      </select>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex gap-2">
                      <input 
                        type="text" 
                        value={settings.ollama_base_url} 
                        onChange={e => setSettings({ ...settings, ollama_base_url: e.target.value })}
                        placeholder="http://localhost:11434"
                        className="flex-1 bg-black/40 border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs text-gray-300 focus:outline-none focus:border-green-500 font-mono"
                      />
                      <button
                        onClick={() => handleTestConnection('ollama', settings.default_model, '', settings.ollama_base_url)}
                        disabled={testingProvider === 'ollama'}
                        className="px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold rounded-xl bg-green-500/10 text-green-400 border border-green-500/30 hover:bg-green-500/20 transition flex items-center gap-1"
                      >
                        {testingProvider === 'ollama' ? <RefreshCw size={12} className="animate-spin" /> : <Check size={12} />}
                        Test
                      </button>
                    </div>

                    {testResult.ollama && (
                      <div className={`p-2.5 rounded-lg text-xs border ${testResult.ollama.success ? 'bg-green-500/10 text-green-300 border-green-500/30' : 'bg-red-500/10 text-red-300 border-red-500/30'}`}>
                        {testResult.ollama.message}
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <div className="p-3 bg-yellow-500/10 border border-yellow-500/20 rounded-xl text-xs text-yellow-300/90 leading-relaxed">
                    💡 <strong>Mẹo cho người dùng mới:</strong> Cài ứng dụng <strong>Ollama</strong> (từ ollama.com) rồi chạy lệnh ví dụ <code>ollama run qwen2.5:3b</code> hoặc mở app lên là hệ thống tự động nhận diện 100%!
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* LM Studio / Local OpenAI Compatible */}
              <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/10 space-y-4">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <span className="font-semibold text-white">LM Studio / vLLM / LocalAI</span>
                  {localStatus?.lm_studio?.available ? (
                    <span className="text-xs px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-green-500/20 text-green-400 border border-green-500/30">
                      Sẵn sàng
                    </span>
                  ) : (
                    <span className="text-xs text-gray-500">Cổng 1234</span>
                  )}
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-gray-400 mb-1">Địa chỉ Local Endpoint:</label>
                    <input 
                      type="text" 
                      value={settings.lm_studio_base_url}
                      onChange={e => setSettings({ ...settings, lm_studio_base_url: e.target.value })}
                      placeholder="http://localhost:1234/v1"
                      className="w-full bg-black/40 border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500 font-mono"
                    />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-gray-400 mb-1">Tên Model tải trong LM Studio:</label>
                    <input 
                      type="text" 
                      value={settings.custom_model || 'local-model'}
                      onChange={e => setSettings({ ...settings, custom_model: e.target.value })}
                      placeholder="local-model"
                      className="w-full bg-black/40 border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500 font-mono"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <button
                  onClick={() => handleTestConnection('lm_studio', settings.custom_model || 'local-model', '', settings.lm_studio_base_url)}
                  disabled={testingProvider === 'lm_studio'}
                  className="w-full py-2 text-xs font-semibold rounded-xl bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 text-gray-200 border border-white/10 transition flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"
                >
                  {testingProvider === 'lm_studio' ? <RefreshCw size={12} className="animate-spin" /> : null}
                  Kiểm tra kết nối LM Studio
                </button>

                {testResult.lm_studio && (
                  <div className={`p-2.5 rounded-lg text-xs border ${testResult.lm_studio.success ? 'bg-green-500/10 text-green-300 border-green-500/30' : 'bg-red-500/10 text-red-300 border-red-500/30'}`}>
                    {testResult.lm_studio.message}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* TAB 2: 9ROUTER GATEWAY */}
          {activeTab === '9router' && (
            <div className="space-y-5 animate-fade-in">
              <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-purple-500/[0.04] border border-purple-500/20 space-y-4">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">

                    <span className="font-semibold text-white">9Router AI Gateway</span>
                    {localStatus?.router9?.available ? (
                      <span className="text-xs px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-green-500/20 text-green-400 border border-green-500/30 flex items-center gap-1">
                        <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-green-400 animate-pulse"></span> Đã kết nối ({localStatus.router9.models.length} model)

                    ) : (
                      <span className="text-xs px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-gray-500/20 text-gray-400 border border-gray-500/30">

                  <ul className="list-disc list-inside space-y-0.5 text-gray-400 text-[11px]">
                    <li><strong>Smart 3-Tier Auto Fallback:</strong> Tự động đổi sang model khác khi bị Rate Limit (429) hoặc hết quota.</li>
                    <li><strong>Hỗ trợ 60+ nhà cung cấp AI</strong> chỉ qua 1 cổng duy nhất tương thích OpenAI API.</li>
                    <li>Cài đặt nhanh trên máy: <code className="bg-white/10 px-1 py-0.5 rounded text-white font-mono">npm install -g 9router && 9router</code></li>
                  </ul>
                </div>

                <div className="space-y-3">
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-gray-300 mb-1">9Router Endpoint URL:</label>
                    <input 
                      type="text" 
                      value={settings.router9_base_url || 'http://localhost:20128/v1'}
                      onChange={e => setSettings({ ...settings, router9_base_url: e.target.value })}
                      placeholder="http://localhost:20128/v1"
                      className="w-full bg-black/40 border border-white/15 rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-purple-500 font-mono"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                    <div className="relative">
                      <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-gray-300 mb-1">9Router API Key (nếu có đặt):</label>
                      <input 
                        type={showKeys.router9 ? "text" : "password"}
                        value={settings.router9_api_key || ''}
                        onChange={e => setSettings({ ...settings, router9_api_key: e.target.value })}
                        placeholder="Lấy từ Dashboard localhost:20128"
                        className="w-full bg-black/40 border border-white/15 rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2.5 pr-10 text-xs text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-purple-500 font-mono"

                        type="button" 
                        onClick={() => toggleShowKey('router9')}

                      >
                        {showKeys.router9 ? <EyeOff size={14} /> : <Eye size={14} />}

                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-gray-300 mb-1">Tên Model định tuyến:</label>
                      <input 
                        type="text" 
                        value={settings.router9_model || 'claude-3-5-sonnet'}
                        onChange={e => setSettings({ ...settings, router9_model: e.target.value })}
                        placeholder="claude-3-5-sonnet, deepseek-chat, gpt-4o"
                        className="w-full bg-black/40 border border-white/15 rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-purple-500 font-mono"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <button
                    onClick={() => handleTestConnection('9router', settings.router9_model || 'claude-3-5-sonnet', settings.router9_api_key, settings.router9_base_url || 'http://localhost:20128/v1')}
                    disabled={testingProvider === '9router'}
                    className="w-full py-2.5 text-xs font-semibold rounded-xl bg-purple-600/20 text-purple-300 border border-purple-500/40 hover:bg-purple-600/30 transition flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"
                  >
                    {testingProvider === '9router' ? <RefreshCw size={13} className="animate-spin" /> : <Check size={13} />}
                    Kiểm tra kết nối 9Router Gateway
                  </button>

                  {testResult['9router'] && (
                    <div className={`p-2.5 rounded-lg text-xs border ${testResult['9router'].success ? 'bg-green-500/10 text-green-300 border-green-500/30' : 'bg-red-500/10 text-red-300 border-red-500/30'}`}>
                      {testResult['9router'].message}
                    </div>
                  )}

              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* TAB 3: CLOUD API */}
          {activeTab === 'cloud' && (
            <div className="space-y-5 animate-fade-in">
              
              {/* Gemini */}
              <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/10 space-y-3">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">

                    </a>
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-xs text-gray-500">Khuyên dùng</span>
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
                  <div className="sm:col-span-2 relative">
                    <input 
                      type={showKeys.gemini ? "text" : "password"}
                      value={settings.gemini_api_key || ''}
                      onChange={e => setSettings({ ...settings, gemini_api_key: e.target.value })}
                      placeholder="AIzaSy..."
                      className="w-full bg-black/40 border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 pr-10 text-xs text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500 font-mono"

                    <button 
                      type="button" 
                      onClick={() => toggleShowKey('gemini')}
                      className="absolute right-3 top-2.5 text-gray-400 hover:text-white"
                    >
                      {showKeys.gemini ? <EyeOff size={14} /> : <Eye size={14} />}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <select
                      value={settings.gemini_model || 'gemini-2.5-flash'}
                      onChange={e => setSettings({ ...settings, gemini_model: e.target.value })}
                      className="w-full bg-[#121316] border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
                    >
                      <option value="gemini-2.5-flash">gemini-2.5-flash (Nhanh)</option>
                      <option value="gemini-2.0-flash">gemini-2.0-flash</option>
                      <option value="gemini-1.5-flash">gemini-1.5-flash</option>
                      <option value="gemini-1.5-pro">gemini-1.5-pro (Mạnh)</option>
                    </select>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <button
                  onClick={() => handleTestConnection('gemini', settings.gemini_model, settings.gemini_api_key, '')}

                >
                  {testingProvider === 'gemini' ? <RefreshCw size={12} className="animate-spin" /> : null}
                  Kiểm tra Gemini Key
                </button>

                {testResult.gemini && (
                  <div className={`p-2.5 rounded-lg text-xs border ${testResult.gemini.success ? 'bg-green-500/10 text-green-300 border-green-500/30' : 'bg-red-500/10 text-red-300 border-red-500/30'}`}>
                    {testResult.gemini.message}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* OpenAI */}
              <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/10 space-y-3">

                  <a href="https://platform.openai.com/api-keys" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" className="text-xs text-blue-400 hover:underline flex items-center gap-0.5">
                    Lấy Key <ExternalLink size={10} />
                  </a>
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
                  <div className="sm:col-span-2 relative">
                    <input 
                      type={showKeys.openai ? "text" : "password"}
                      value={settings.openai_api_key || ''}
                      onChange={e => setSettings({ ...settings, openai_api_key: e.target.value })}
                      placeholder="sk-proj-..."
                      className="w-full bg-black/40 border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 pr-10 text-xs text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500 font-mono"
                    />
                    <button 
                      type="button" 
                      onClick={() => toggleShowKey('openai')}
                      className="absolute right-3 top-2.5 text-gray-400 hover:text-white"
                    >
                      {showKeys.openai ? <EyeOff size={14} /> : <Eye size={14} />}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <select
                      value={settings.openai_model || 'gpt-4o-mini'}
                      onChange={e => setSettings({ ...settings, openai_model: e.target.value })}
                      className="w-full bg-[#121316] border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
                    >
                      <option value="gpt-4o-mini">gpt-4o-mini (Tiết kiệm)</option>
                      <option value="gpt-4o">gpt-4o (Thông minh)</option>
                      <option value="gpt-3.5-turbo">gpt-3.5-turbo</option>
                    </select>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <button
                  onClick={() => handleTestConnection('openai', settings.openai_model, settings.openai_api_key, '')}
                  disabled={testingProvider === 'openai' || !settings.openai_api_key}
                  className="px-4 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold rounded-lg bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-400 border border-blue-500/30 hover:bg-blue-500/20 disabled:opacity-40 transition flex items-center gap-1.5"
                >
                  {testingProvider === 'openai' ? <RefreshCw size={12} className="animate-spin" /> : null}
                  Kiểm tra OpenAI Key
                </button>

                {testResult.openai && (
                  <div className={`p-2.5 rounded-lg text-xs border ${testResult.openai.success ? 'bg-green-500/10 text-green-300 border-green-500/30' : 'bg-red-500/10 text-red-300 border-red-500/30'}`}>
                    {testResult.openai.message}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* Groq (Fast & Free) */}
              <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/10 space-y-3">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="font-semibold text-white">Groq</span>
                    <span className="text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-purple-500/20 text-purple-300 font-semibold">Cực nhanh</span>
                  </div>
                  <a href="https://console.groq.com/keys" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" className="text-xs text-blue-400 hover:underline flex items-center gap-0.5">
                    Lấy Key miễn phí <ExternalLink size={10} />
                  </a>
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
                  <div className="sm:col-span-2 relative">
                    <input 
                      type={showKeys.groq ? "text" : "password"}
                      value={settings.groq_api_key || ''}
                      onChange={e => setSettings({ ...settings, groq_api_key: e.target.value })}
                      placeholder="gsk_..."
                      className="w-full bg-black/40 border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 pr-10 text-xs text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-purple-500 font-mono"
                    />
                    <button 
                      type="button" 
                      onClick={() => toggleShowKey('groq')}
                      className="absolute right-3 top-2.5 text-gray-400 hover:text-white"
                    >
                      {showKeys.groq ? <EyeOff size={14} /> : <Eye size={14} />}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <select
                      value={settings.groq_model || 'llama-3.3-70b-versatile'}
                      onChange={e => setSettings({ ...settings, groq_model: e.target.value })}
                      className="w-full bg-[#121316] border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-purple-500"
                    >
                      <option value="llama-3.3-70b-versatile">llama-3.3-70b</option>
                      <option value="llama-3.1-8b-instant">llama-3.1-8b (Flash)</option>
                      <option value="mixtral-8x7b-32768">mixtral-8x7b</option>
                    </select>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <button
                  onClick={() => handleTestConnection('groq', settings.groq_model, settings.groq_api_key, '')}
                  disabled={testingProvider === 'groq' || !settings.groq_api_key}
                  className="px-4 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold rounded-lg bg-purple-500/10 text-purple-400 border border-purple-500/30 hover:bg-purple-500/20 disabled:opacity-40 transition flex items-center gap-1.5"
                >
                  {testingProvider === 'groq' ? <RefreshCw size={12} className="animate-spin" /> : null}
                  Kiểm tra Groq Key
                </button>

                {testResult.groq && (
                  <div className={`p-2.5 rounded-lg text-xs border ${testResult.groq.success ? 'bg-green-500/10 text-green-300 border-green-500/30' : 'bg-red-500/10 text-red-300 border-red-500/30'}`}>
                    {testResult.groq.message}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

            </div>
          )}

        </div>

        {/* Footer */}
        <div className="p-4 border-t border-white/10 bg-white/[0.02] flex items-center justify-between">
          <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">Cài đặt được lưu trữ an toàn trong máy tính của bạn.</p>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <button
              onClick={onClose}
              className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-medium text-gray-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/5 transition"
            >
              Đóng
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={handleSave}
              disabled={loading}
              className="px-5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold bg-white text-black hover:bg-gray-200 transition shadow-lg flex items-center gap-1.5 disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {loading ? <RefreshCw size={14} className="animate-spin" /> : <Check size={14} />}
              Lưu Cài Đặt
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


# ==============================================================================
# FILE: frontend/src/components/ShareModal.jsx
# ==============================================================================

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { X, Share2, Copy, Check, Download, BookOpen, Link, FileJson } from 'lucide-react';

export default function ShareModal({ isOpen, onClose, notebook, studyGuide, onExportJson, isDark = true }) {
  const [copiedLink, setCopiedLink] = useState(false);
  const [copiedSummary, setCopiedSummary] = useState(false);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const currentUrl = window.location.href;

  const handleCopyLink = () => {
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(currentUrl);
    setCopiedLink(true);
    setTimeout(() => setCopiedLink(false), 2000);
  };

  const handleCopySummary = () => {
    if (!studyGuide) return;
    const text = `# ${notebook?.title || "NotebookLM Sổ tay"}\n\n## Tóm tắt Cốt lõi\n${studyGuide.summary || ""}\n\n## Thuật ngữ Quan trọng\n${studyGuide.glossary || ""}\n\n## Câu hỏi ôn tập\n${studyGuide.faq || ""}`;
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
    setCopiedSummary(true);
    setTimeout(() => setCopiedSummary(false), 2000);
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-md p-4 animate-fade-in font-sans">
      <div className={`w-full max-w-lg rounded-3xl shadow-2xl border overflow-hidden p-6 transition-colors ${
        isDark ? 'bg-[#181a20] border-white/15 text-white' : 'bg-white border-gray-200 text-gray-900'
      }`}>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-4 border-b border-white/10">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <div className="p-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-400 border border-blue-500/30">
              <Share2 size={18} />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-base font-bold">Chia sẻ & Xuất Sổ tay</h3>
              <p className={`text-xs ${isDark ? 'text-gray-400' : 'text-gray-500'}`}>{notebook?.title || "Sổ tay nghiên cứu"}</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button 
            onClick={onClose}
            className="text-gray-400 hover:text-white p-1 rounded-lg hover:bg-white/5 transition"
          >
            <X size={18} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-4 my-5">
          {/* Option 1: Share Link */}
          <div className="p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/10 space-y-2">
            <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-300 flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <Link size={14} className="text-blue-400" /> Liên kết trực tiếp tới Sổ tay:
            </span>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <input
                type="text"
                readOnly
                value={currentUrl}
                className="w-full bg-black/40 text-xs px-3 py-2 rounded-xl text-gray-300 font-mono border border-white/10 outline-none select-all"
              />
              <button
                onClick={handleCopyLink}
                className="px-3 py-2 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold transition shrink-0 flex items-center gap-1"
              >
                {copiedLink ? <Check size={14} /> : <Copy size={14} />}
                <span>{copiedLink ? "Đã chép" : "Chép link"}</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Option 2: Export JSON Bundle */}
          <div className="p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/10 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <div>
              <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-200 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <FileJson size={14} className="text-green-400" /> Xuất bản sao lưu dữ liệu (.json)
              </div>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-400 mt-0.5">
                Bao gồm tài liệu, lịch sử chat, cẩm nang, thẻ Quizlet & tiến độ học.
              </p>
            </div>
            <button
              onClick={onExportJson}
              className="px-3.5 py-2 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold transition shrink-0 flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow"
            >
              <Download size={14} />
              <span>Tải .json</span>
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Option 3: Copy Study Guide Markdown */}
          {studyGuide && (
            <div className="p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/10 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
              <div>
                <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-200 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <BookOpen size={14} className="text-purple-400" /> Sao chép Cẩm nang tóm tắt
                </div>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-400 mt-0.5">
                  Dạng văn bản Markdown hoàn chỉnh để gửi qua tin nhắn hoặc in ấn.
                </p>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={handleCopySummary}
                className="px-3.5 py-2 bg-purple-600 hover:bg-purple-500 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold transition shrink-0 flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow"
              >
                {copiedSummary ? <Check size={14} /> : <Copy size={14} />}
                <span>{copiedSummary ? "Đã chép" : "Chép tóm tắt"}</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="pt-2 flex justify-end">
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/20 text-white px-5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold transition"
          >
            Đóng
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import React from 'react';
import { X, Command, Keyboard } from 'lucide-react';

export default function ShortcutsModal({ isOpen, onClose, isDark = true }) {
  if (!isOpen) return null;

    </div>
  );
}

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { X, Clock, ListTree, RefreshCw, Sparkles, MessageSquare, ChevronDown, ChevronRight, Tag } from 'lucide-react';

export default function TimelineOutlineModal({ 
  isOpen, 
  onClose, 
  data, 
  onRegenerate, 
  loading, 
  onAskAboutTopic, 
  isDark = true 
}) {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('timeline'); // 'timeline' | 'outline'
  const [focusTopic, setFocusTopic] = useState('');
  const [expandedSections, setExpandedSections] = useState({ 0: true, 1: true });

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const timelineItems = data?.timeline || [];
  const outlineItems = data?.outline || [];

  const toggleSection = (idx) => {
    setExpandedSections(prev => ({ ...prev, [idx]: !prev[idx] }));
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-md p-4 animate-fade-in font-sans">
      <div className={`w-full max-w-4xl h-[88vh] flex flex-col rounded-3xl shadow-2xl border overflow-hidden transition-colors ${
        isDark ? 'bg-[#14151a] border-white/15 text-white' : 'bg-white border-gray-200 text-gray-900'
      }`}>
        {/* Header */}
        <div className={`p-4 border-b flex items-center justify-between ${
          isDark ? 'bg-white/[0.02] border-white/10' : 'bg-gray-50 border-gray-200'
        }`}>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="p-2 bg-gradient-to-tr from-emerald-500 to-teal-600 rounded-xl text-white shadow-md">
              <Clock size={20} />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-base font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
                Dòng thời gian & Dàn ý Bài học
                <span className="text-[11px] font-normal px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-400 border border-emerald-500/30">
                  Timeline & Outline
                </span>
              </h2>
              <p className={`text-xs ${isDark ? 'text-gray-400' : 'text-gray-500'}`}>
                Trích xuất niên biểu tiến trình và dàn ý phân cấp có hệ thống từ tài liệu
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className={`p-2 rounded-xl transition ${isDark ? 'hover:bg-white/10 text-gray-400 hover:text-white' : 'hover:bg-gray-100 text-gray-500 hover:text-black'}`}
          >
            <X size={20} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Action & Tab Header */}
        <div className={`px-5 py-3 border-b flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 ${
          isDark ? 'bg-black/20 border-white/10' : 'bg-gray-50/70 border-gray-200'
        }`}>
          {/* Tab Switcher */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-white/5 p-1 rounded-xl border border-white/10">
            <button
              onClick={() => setActiveTab('timeline')}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition ${
                activeTab === 'timeline' 
                  ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white shadow' 
                  : 'text-gray-400 hover:text-white'
              }`}
            >
              <Clock size={14} />
              <span>Dòng thời gian ({timelineItems.length})</span>
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => setActiveTab('outline')}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition ${
                activeTab === 'outline' 
                  ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white shadow' 
                  : 'text-gray-400 hover:text-white'
              }`}
            >
              <ListTree size={14} />
              <span>Dàn ý phân cấp ({outlineItems.length})</span>
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Regenerate controls */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-1 max-w-md justify-end">
            <input
              type="text"
              value={focusTopic}
              onChange={e => setFocusTopic(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Chủ đề trọng tâm cần tập trung..."
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs border outline-none w-full max-w-[220px] transition ${
                isDark ? 'bg-white/5 border-white/10 text-white focus:border-emerald-500' : 'bg-white border-gray-200 text-black'
              }`}
            />
            <button
              onClick={() => onRegenerate(focusTopic)}
              disabled={loading}
              className="bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-600 to-teal-600 hover:opacity-90 disabled:opacity-50 text-white px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold shadow transition flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0"
            >
              <RefreshCw size={12} className={loading ? "animate-spin" : ""} />
              <span>{loading ? "Đang tạo..." : "Tái tạo"}</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Content Body */}
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6 scrollbar-hide">
          {loading ? (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full gap-3 text-gray-400">
              <RefreshCw size={30} className="animate-spin text-emerald-400" />
              <p className="text-sm font-medium">AI đang trích xuất mốc sự kiện và xây dựng khung dàn ý...</p>
            </div>
          ) : activeTab === 'timeline' ? (
            /* Timeline View */
            timelineItems.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full gap-2 text-gray-400">
                <Clock size={36} className="text-gray-500" />
                <p className="text-sm">Chưa có dữ liệu dòng thời gian. Nhấn "Tái tạo" để bắt đầu.</p>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="relative pl-6 space-y-6 max-w-2xl mx-auto before:absolute before:left-2 before:top-2 before:bottom-2 before:w-0.5 before:bg-gradient-to-b before:from-emerald-500 before:via-teal-500 before:to-emerald-500/20">
                {timelineItems.map((item, idx) => (
                  <div key={idx} className="relative group">
                    {/* Node Dot */}
                    <div className="absolute -left-[27px] top-1.5 w-4 h-4 rounded-full bg-emerald-500 border-4 border-[#14151a] shadow-md group-hover:scale-125 transition"></div>

                    {/* Card */}
                    <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/10 hover:border-emerald-500/40 transition shadow-sm space-y-1.5">
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                        <span className="text-[11px] font-mono font-bold text-emerald-400 uppercase tracking-wider">
                          {item.period || `Mốc ${idx + 1}`}
                        </span>
                        {item.tag && (
                          <span className="text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-500/20 flex items-center gap-1">
                            <Tag size={10} />
                            {item.tag}
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </div>

                      <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-white group-hover:text-emerald-300 transition">
                        {item.title}
                      </h4>

                      <p className="text-xs text-gray-300 leading-relaxed pt-1">
                        {item.description}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            )
          ) : (
            /* Outline View */
            outlineItems.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full gap-2 text-gray-400">
                <ListTree size={36} className="text-gray-500" />
                <p className="text-sm">Chưa có dàn ý bài học. Nhấn "Tái tạo" để bắt đầu.</p>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="space-y-4 max-w-2xl mx-auto">
                {outlineItems.map((section, idx) => {
                  const isExpanded = !!expandedSections[idx];
                  return (
                    <div 
                      key={section.id || idx}
                      className="rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.03] overflow-hidden"
                    >
                      {/* Section Header */}
                      <div 
                        onClick={() => toggleSection(idx)}
                        className="p-4 flex items-center justify-between cursor-pointer hover:bg-white/5 transition"
                      >
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                          {isExpanded ? <ChevronDown size={18} className="text-emerald-400" /> : <ChevronRight size={18} className="text-gray-400" />}
                          <div>
                            <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-white">{section.title}</h4>
                            {section.summary && (
                              <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-0.5 line-clamp-1">{section.summary}</p>
                            )}
                          </div>
                        </div>

                        <button
                          onClick={(e) => {
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            onAskAboutTopic(`Hãy giải thích chi tiết và đưa ra ví dụ về: "${section.title}"`);
                            onClose();
                          }}
                          className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold bg-emerald-500/10 hover:bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-500/30 flex items-center gap-1.5 transition shrink-0"
                          title="Gửi câu hỏi này sang khung trò chuyện AI"
                        >
                          <MessageSquare size={12} />
                          <span>Hỏi AI</span>
                        </button>
                      </div>

                      {/* Subsections */}
                      {isExpanded && section.subsections && (
                        <div className="p-4 pt-1 border-t border-white/10 space-y-3 bg-black/20">
                          {section.subsections.map((sub, sIdx) => (
                            <div key={sub.id || sIdx} className="pl-3 border-l-2 border-emerald-500/40 space-y-1">
                              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                                <h5 className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-200">{sub.title}</h5>
                                <button
                                  onClick={() => {
                                    onAskAboutTopic(`Phân tích chuyên sâu về: "${sub.title}"`);
                                    onClose();
                                  }}
                                  className="text-[11px] text-emerald-400 hover:underline flex items-center gap-1"
                                >
                                  <MessageSquare size={11} /> Hỏi mục này
                                </button>
                              </div>
                              {sub.points && sub.points.length > 0 && (
                                <ul className="list-disc list-inside text-xs text-gray-400 space-y-0.5 pl-1">
                                  {sub.points.map((pt, pIdx) => (
                                    <li key={pIdx}>{pt}</li>
                                  ))}
                                </ul>
                              )}
                            </div>
                          ))}
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            )
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
