# ==============================================================================
# FILE: frontend/src/pages/Dashboard.jsx
# ==============================================================================

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { BookOpen, Plus, Search, Check, Settings, Trash2, Cpu, Cloud, Server, ExternalLink, Sun, Moon, Upload, FileJson } from 'lucide-react';
import axios from 'axios';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'motion/react';
import { spring, transition, blur } from '@animate-ui/react/utils/motion';
import SettingsModal from '../components/SettingsModal';

function Dashboard() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [notebooks, setNotebooks] = useState([]);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [showModal, setShowModal] = useState(false);
  const [showSettings, setShowSettings] = useState(false);

  // Theme state
  const [theme, setTheme] = useState(() => localStorage.getItem('notebooklm_theme') || 'dark');
  const toggleTheme = () => {
    const next = theme === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark';
    setTheme(next);
    localStorage.setItem('notebooklm_theme', next);
  };
  const isDark = theme === 'dark';

  // Import Notebook state
  const importInputRef = useRef(null);
  const [importing, setImporting] = useState(false);

  const handleImportFile = async (e) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    setImporting(true);
    try {
      const text = await file.text();
      const parsed = JSON.parse(text);
      const res = await axios.post('/api/notebooks/import', parsed);
      if (res.data && res.data.notebook_id) {
        alert("Nhập sổ tay thành công!");
        fetchNotebooks();
        navigate(`/notebook/${res.data.notebook_id}`);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      alert("Lỗi nhập sổ tay: " + (err.response?.data?.detail || err.message));
    } finally {
      setImporting(false);
      if (importInputRef.current) importInputRef.current.value = "";
    }
  };

  // Create Notebook Form State
  const [newTitle, setNewTitle] = useState('');
  const [provider, setProvider] = useState('ollama'); // 'ollama', 'lm_studio', 'gemini', 'openai', 'groq'
  const [selectedModel, setSelectedModel] = useState('');
  const [customKey, setCustomKey] = useState('');
  const [globalSettings, setGlobalSettings] = useState({});
  const [localModels, setLocalModels] = useState([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchNotebooks();
    fetchSettingsAndModels();
  }, []);

  const fetchNotebooks = async () => {
    try {
      const res = await axios.get('/api/notebooks');
      setNotebooks(res.data);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
    }
  };

  const fetchSettingsAndModels = async () => {
    try {
      const [settingsRes, modelsRes] = await Promise.all([
        axios.get('/api/settings'),
        axios.get('/api/llm/local-models')
      ]);
      setGlobalSettings(settingsRes.data || {});
      const ollamaModels = modelsRes.data?.ollama?.models || [];
      setLocalModels(ollamaModels);
      if (ollamaModels.length > 0 && !selectedModel) {
        setSelectedModel(ollamaModels[0].name);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
    }
  };

  const handleOpenCreateModal = () => {
    fetchSettingsAndModels();
    setNewTitle('');
    setCustomKey('');
    setShowModal(true);
  };

  const handleDeleteNotebook = async (e, id) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    if (!window.confirm("Bạn có chắc chắn muốn xóa Sổ tay này cùng toàn bộ tài liệu bên trong không?")) return;
    try {
      await axios.delete(`/api/notebooks/${id}`);
      setNotebooks(prev => prev.filter(nb => nb.id !== id));
    } catch (err) {
      alert("Lỗi xóa sổ tay: " + (err.response?.data?.detail || err.message));
    }
  };

  const handleCreate = async () => {
    if (!newTitle.trim()) return;

    let finalKey = customKey.trim();
    if (!finalKey) {
      if (provider === 'gemini') finalKey = globalSettings.gemini_api_key || '';
      else if (provider === 'openai') finalKey = globalSettings.openai_api_key || '';
      else if (provider === 'groq') finalKey = globalSettings.groq_api_key || '';
      else if (provider === '9router') finalKey = globalSettings.router9_api_key || '9router-local';
    }

    if (['gemini', 'openai', 'groq'].includes(provider) && !finalKey) {
      alert(`Vui lòng nhập API Key cho ${provider.toUpperCase()} hoặc cài đặt trong phần Cài đặt!`);
      return;
    }
    
    const id = Date.now().toString();
    const isPrivate = ['ollama', 'lm_studio'].includes(provider);

    try {
      await axios.post('/api/notebooks', {
        id,
        title: newTitle.trim(),
        is_private: isPrivate,
        provider,
        model_name: selectedModel || undefined,
        api_key: finalKey || undefined
      });
      setShowModal(false);
      navigate(`/notebook/${id}`);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      alert('Lỗi tạo sổ ghi chú: ' + (err.response?.data?.detail || err.message));
    }
  };

  const filteredNotebooks = notebooks.filter(nb => 
    nb.title.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase())
  );

  const getProviderBadge = (nb) => {
    const prov = (nb.provider || (nb.is_private ? 'ollama' : 'gemini')).toLowerCase();
    if (prov === 'ollama') {
      return {
        label: `Ollama: ${nb.model_name || 'qwen2.5:3b'}`,
        color: 'bg-green-500/20 text-green-400 border-green-500/30'
      };
    }
    if (prov === 'lm_studio') {
      return {
        label: `LM Studio: ${nb.model_name || 'Local'}`,
        color: 'bg-teal-500/20 text-teal-300 border-teal-500/30'
      };
    }
    if (prov === '9router') {
      return {
        label: `9Router: ${nb.model_name || 'Multi-model'}`,
        color: 'bg-orange-500/20 text-orange-400 border-orange-500/30'
      };
    }
    if (prov === 'gemini') {
      return {
        label: `Gemini: ${nb.model_name || 'Flash'}`,
        color: 'bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-400 border-blue-500/30'
      };
    }
    if (prov === 'groq') {
      return {
        label: `Groq: ${nb.model_name || 'Llama'}`,
        color: 'bg-purple-500/20 text-purple-400 border-purple-500/30'
      };
    }
    return {
      label: `${prov.toUpperCase()}: ${nb.model_name || ''}`,
      color: 'bg-gray-500/20 text-gray-300 border-gray-500/30'
    };
  };

  return (
    <div className={`flex flex-col min-h-screen font-sans w-full transition-colors ${
      isDark ? 'bg-[#121316] text-white selection:bg-blue-500/30' : 'bg-[#f8fafc] text-slate-900 selection:bg-blue-200'
    }`}>
      {/* Hidden file input for notebook import */}
      <input
        type="file"
        ref={importInputRef}
        onChange={handleImportFile}
        accept=".json"
        className="hidden"
      />

      {/* Header */}
      <header className={`flex justify-between items-center px-6 py-4 border-b transition-colors ${
        isDark ? 'border-white/5 bg-[#14161a]' : 'border-slate-200 bg-white'
      }`}>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 cursor-pointer" onClick={() => navigate('/')}>
          <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-blue-600 flex items-center justify-center text-white font-bold shadow-md">
            <BookOpen size={18} />
          </div>
          <span className="font-semibold text-lg tracking-tight">NotebookLM Mini</span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          {/* Theme Switcher */}
          <button 
            onClick={toggleTheme}
            className={`w-9 h-9 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center transition border ${
              isDark ? 'border-white/10 bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 text-amber-400' : 'border-slate-200 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700'
            }`}
            title={isDark ? "Chuyển sang giao diện Sáng" : "Chuyển sang giao diện Tối"}
          >
            {isDark ? <Sun size={17} /> : <Moon size={17} />}
          </button>

          <button 
            onClick={() => setShowSettings(true)}
            className={`flex items-center gap-2 border px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-sm font-medium transition ${
              isDark ? 'border-white/10 bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 text-gray-200' : 'border-slate-200 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700'
            }`}
          >
            <Settings size={16} /> Cài đặt Model & API Key
          </button>
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* Main Area */}
      <main className="flex-1 max-w-6xl w-full mx-auto p-8">
        
        {/* Toolbar */}
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4 mb-8">
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight">Sổ ghi chú của bạn</h1>
            <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1">Quản lý và nghiên cứu thông tin dựa trên dữ liệu của riêng bạn</p>
          </div>
          
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 w-full sm:w-auto">
            <div className="relative flex-1 sm:w-64">
              <Search size={16} className="absolute left-3.5 top-3 text-gray-400" />
              <input 
                type="text" 
                placeholder="Tìm kiếm sổ ghi chú..." 
                value={searchQuery}
                onChange={e => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
                className={`w-full border rounded-full pl-9 pr-4 py-2 text-sm placeholder-gray-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500 ${
                  isDark ? 'bg-white/5 border-white/10 text-white' : 'bg-white border-slate-200 text-slate-800'
                }`}
              />
            </div>

            {/* Import Notebook Button */}
            <motion.button 
              type="button"
              whileHover={{ scale: 1.02 }}
              whileTap={{ scale: 0.97 }}
              transition={spring.press}
              onClick={() => importInputRef.current?.click()}
              disabled={importing}
              className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 rounded-full font-semibold text-xs border transition shadow shrink-0 cursor-pointer ${
                isDark ? 'bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 border-white/15 text-gray-200' : 'bg-white hover:bg-slate-100 border-slate-300 text-slate-700'
              }`}
              title="Nhập sổ tay từ file backup JSON"
            >
              <Upload size={15} />
              <span>{importing ? "Đang nhập..." : "Nhập .json"}</span>
            </motion.button>

            {/* Create Notebook Button */}
            <motion.button 
              type="button"
              whileHover={{ scale: 1.03 }}
              whileTap={{ scale: 0.97 }}
              transition={spring.press}
              onClick={handleOpenCreateModal}
              className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-blue-600 text-white hover:bg-blue-500 px-5 py-2 rounded-full font-semibold text-sm transition-colors shadow-lg shrink-0 cursor-pointer"
            >
              <Plus size={18} /> Tạo mới
            </motion.button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Notebooks Grid */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-6">
          {/* Create New Card */}
          <motion.div 
            whileHover={{ y: -4 }}
            whileTap={{ scale: 0.985 }}
            transition={spring.highlight}
            onClick={handleOpenCreateModal}
            className={`aspect-[4/3] rounded-2xl border border-dashed transition-colors cursor-pointer flex flex-col items-center justify-center group ${
              isDark 
                ? 'bg-white/[0.03] hover:bg-white/[0.07] border-white/15 hover:border-white/30' 
                : 'bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 border-slate-300 hover:border-slate-400 shadow-sm'
            }`}
          >
            <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-500 flex items-center justify-center mb-4 group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform">
              <Plus size={24} />
            </div>
            <span className={`font-semibold text-base ${isDark ? 'text-gray-200' : 'text-slate-800'}`}>Tạo sổ ghi chú mới</span>
            <span className={`text-xs mt-1 ${isDark ? 'text-gray-500' : 'text-slate-400'}`}>Cắm Local hoặc Cloud AI</span>
          </motion.div>

          {/* Existing Notebooks */}
          {filteredNotebooks.map(nb => {
            const badge = getProviderBadge(nb);
            return (
              <motion.div 
                key={nb.id}
                whileHover={{ y: -4 }}
                whileTap={{ scale: 0.985 }}
                transition={spring.highlight}
                onClick={() => navigate(`/notebook/${nb.id}`)}
                className={`aspect-[4/3] rounded-2xl border p-5 flex flex-col justify-between cursor-pointer transition-shadow hover:shadow-xl relative overflow-hidden group ${
                  isDark 
                    ? 'bg-[#181a20] border-white/10 hover:border-white/25 text-white' 
                    : 'bg-white border-slate-200 hover:border-slate-300 text-slate-800 shadow-sm'
                }`}
              >
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <span className={`text-[11px] font-medium px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full border ${badge.color}`}>
                    {badge.label}
                  </span>
                  <button 
                    onClick={(e) => handleDeleteNotebook(e, nb.id)}
                    className={`opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 p-1.5 rounded-lg transition ${
                      isDark ? 'text-gray-400 hover:text-red-400 hover:bg-white/10' : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-red-500 hover:bg-slate-100'
                    }`}
                    title="Xóa sổ ghi chú"
                  >
                    <Trash2 size={15} />
                  </button>
                </div>
                
                <div>
                  <h3 className={`font-semibold text-lg mb-1.5 line-clamp-2 leading-snug ${isDark ? 'text-white' : 'text-slate-900'}`}>{nb.title}</h3>
                  <p className={`text-xs ${isDark ? 'text-gray-500' : 'text-slate-400'}`}>{new Date(nb.created_at).toLocaleDateString('vi-VN')}</p>
                </div>
              </motion.div>
            );
          })}
        </div>

      </main>

      {/* Create Modal */}
      <AnimatePresence>
        {showModal && (
          <motion.div 
            initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
            transition={transition.overlay}
            className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/80 flex items-center justify-center z-50 backdrop-blur-sm p-4"
            onClick={() => setShowModal(false)}
          >
            <motion.div 
              initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.94, y: 10, filter: blur.surface }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1, y: 0, filter: 'blur(0px)' }}
              exit={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.94, y: 10, filter: blur.surface }}
              transition={spring.dialog}
              onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
              className="bg-[#181a20] border border-white/10 rounded-2xl p-6 max-w-lg w-full shadow-2xl space-y-5"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 border-b border-white/10">
                <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-white">Tạo Sổ ghi chú mới</h3>
                <button onClick={() => setShowModal(false)} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-white">✕</button>
              </div>
            
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-gray-300 mb-1.5">Tên sổ ghi chú</label>
              <input 
                type="text" 
                value={newTitle}
                onChange={e => setNewTitle(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full bg-black/40 border border-white/15 rounded-xl px-4 py-2.5 text-sm text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
                placeholder="VD: Nghiên cứu sinh học, Ôn thi Luật..."
                autoFocus
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-gray-300 mb-2">Chọn Bộ não AI (Mô hình)</label>
              
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 mb-3">
                <div 
                  onClick={() => {
                    setProvider('ollama');
                    if (localModels.length > 0) setSelectedModel(localModels[0].name);
                  }}
                  className={`p-3 rounded-xl border cursor-pointer transition-all ${
                    provider === 'ollama' 
                      ? 'border-green-500 bg-green-500/10' 
                      : 'border-white/10 bg-white/[0.02] hover:border-white/20'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
                    <Server size={16} className="text-green-400" />
                    <span className="font-semibold text-sm text-green-300">Ollama Local</span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-400 leading-tight">100% Offline trên máy tính, bảo mật tuyệt đối.</p>
                </div>

                <div 
                  onClick={() => {
                    setProvider('gemini');
                    setSelectedModel(globalSettings.gemini_model || 'gemini-2.5-flash');
                  }}
                  className={`p-3 rounded-xl border cursor-pointer transition-all ${
                    provider === 'gemini' 
                      ? 'border-blue-500 bg-blue-500/10' 
                      : 'border-white/10 bg-white/[0.02] hover:border-white/20'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
                    <Cloud size={16} className="text-blue-400" />
                    <span className="font-semibold text-sm text-blue-300">Google Gemini</span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-400 leading-tight">Siêu nhanh, thông minh, miễn phí API key.</p>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Provider Options Row */}
              <div className="flex gap-2 mb-3">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setProvider('openai');
                    setSelectedModel(globalSettings.openai_model || 'gpt-4o-mini');
                  }}
                  className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium border transition ${
                    provider === 'openai' ? 'border-white bg-white/10 text-white' : 'border-white/10 text-gray-400 hover:text-white'
                  }`}
                >
                  OpenAI (ChatGPT)
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setProvider('groq');
                    setSelectedModel(globalSettings.groq_model || 'llama-3.3-70b-versatile');
                  }}
                  className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium border transition ${
                    provider === 'groq' ? 'border-purple-500 bg-purple-500/10 text-purple-300' : 'border-white/10 text-gray-400 hover:text-white'
                  }`}
                >
                  Groq (Siêu tốc)
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setProvider('lm_studio');
                    setSelectedModel('local-model');
                  }}
                  className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium border transition ${
                    provider === 'lm_studio' ? 'border-teal-500 bg-teal-500/10 text-teal-300' : 'border-white/10 text-gray-400 hover:text-white'
                  }`}
                >
                  LM Studio
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setProvider('9router');
                    setSelectedModel('gemini-2.5-flash');
                  }}
                  className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium border transition ${
                    provider === '9router' ? 'border-orange-500 bg-orange-500/10 text-orange-300' : 'border-white/10 text-gray-400 hover:text-white'
                  }`}
                >
                  9Router Gateway
                </button>
              </div>

              {/* Model selection based on provider */}
              {provider === '9router' && (
                <div className="bg-black/30 p-3 rounded-xl border border-white/10 space-y-2">
                  <label className="block text-xs text-orange-300 font-medium">Chọn Model trong 9Router (Mới nhất):</label>
                  <select
                    value={selectedModel}
                    onChange={e => setSelectedModel(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full bg-[#121316] border border-white/15 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-orange-500"
                  >
                    <option value="kr/claude-sonnet-4.5">kr/claude-sonnet-4.5 (Claude 3.7 / 4.5 Sonnet ⭐)</option>
                    <option value="kr/deepseek-3.2">kr/deepseek-3.2 (DeepSeek V3.2 🔥 Mới nhất)</option>
                    <option value="kr/auto">kr/auto (Tự động định tuyến tối ưu)</option>
                    <option value="kr/glm-5">kr/glm-5 (GLM 5.0)</option>
                    <option value="kr/qwen3-coder-next">kr/qwen3-coder-next (Qwen 3 Coder Next)</option>
                    <option value="gemini-2.5-flash">gemini-2.5-flash (Google Gemini)</option>
                  </select>
                </div>
              )}

              {provider === 'ollama' && (
                <div className="bg-black/30 p-3 rounded-xl border border-white/10 space-y-2">
                  <label className="block text-xs text-gray-400 font-medium">Chọn mô hình trong Ollama:</label>
                  {localModels.length > 0 ? (
                    <select
                      value={selectedModel}
                      onChange={e => setSelectedModel(e.target.value)}
                      className="w-full bg-[#121316] border border-white/15 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-green-500"
                    >
                      {localModels.map(m => (
                        <option key={m.name} value={m.name}>
                          {m.name} ({m.size_gb} GB)
                        </option>
                      ))}
                    </select>
                  ) : (
                    <input 
                      type="text" 
                      value={selectedModel} 
                      onChange={e => setSelectedModel(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="VD: qwen2.5:3b hoặc llama3.2"
                      className="w-full bg-black/40 border border-white/15 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-green-500"
                    />
                  )}
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-500">
                    {localModels.length > 0 ? `Đã phát hiện ${localModels.length} mô hình sẵn có trên máy.` : 'Không tìm thấy Ollama đang chạy, bạn vẫn có thể nhập tên model.'}
                  </p>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Cloud API Key field */}
              {['gemini', 'openai', 'groq'].includes(provider) && (
                <div className="bg-black/30 p-3 rounded-xl border border-white/10 space-y-2">
                  <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
                    <label className="text-xs text-gray-300 font-medium">
                      {provider.toUpperCase()} API Key:
                    </label>
                    {globalSettings[`${provider}_api_key`] && !customKey && (
                      <span className="text-[11px] text-green-400 flex items-center gap-1">
                        <Check size={12} /> Đã có Key trong Cài đặt
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <input 
                    type="password" 
                    value={customKey}
                    onChange={e => setCustomKey(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder={globalSettings[`${provider}_api_key`] ? "Dùng key đã lưu (hoặc dán key khác vào đây)" : "Dán API Key vào đây..."}
                    className="w-full bg-black/50 border border-white/15 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500 font-mono"
                  />
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 pt-3 border-t border-white/10">
              <button 
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setShowModal(false)}
                className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-medium text-gray-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/5 transition cursor-pointer"
              >
                Hủy
              </button>
              <motion.button 
                type="button"
                whileHover={{ scale: 1.02 }}
                whileTap={{ scale: 0.98 }}
                transition={spring.press}
                onClick={handleCreate}
                disabled={!newTitle.trim()}
                className="px-5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold bg-white text-black hover:bg-gray-200 disabled:opacity-50 transition shadow-lg cursor-pointer"
              >
                Tạo sổ ghi chú
              </motion.button>
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        </motion.div>
      )}
    </AnimatePresence>

      {/* Global Settings Modal */}
      <SettingsModal 
        isOpen={showSettings} 
        onClose={() => setShowSettings(false)} 
        onSettingsSaved={() => {
          fetchSettingsAndModels();
        }}
      />

    </div>
  );
}

export default Dashboard;


# ==============================================================================
# FILE: frontend/src/pages/Landing.jsx
# ==============================================================================

import React from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { BookOpen } from 'lucide-react';

function Landing() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen w-screen flex flex-col bg-[#F9F9FB] text-gray-900 font-sans selection:bg-blue-200">
      
      {/* Header */}
      <header className="flex items-center justify-between px-8 py-4 bg-white border-b border-gray-200">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <BookOpen className="text-gray-700" size={24} />
          <span className="font-semibold text-xl tracking-tight text-gray-800">NotebookLM Mini</span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-6 text-sm font-medium text-gray-600">
          <button className="hover:text-black transition-colors border-b-2 border-black pb-1">Tổng quan</button>
          <button className="hover:text-black transition-colors">Gói</button>
          <button className="hover:text-black transition-colors">Tải ứng dụng</button>
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* Main Content */}
      <main className="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center px-4 animate-fade-in">
        <h1 className="text-6xl md:text-8xl font-medium tracking-tight mb-6">
          Tìm hiểu <span className="text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-500 to-green-400">mọi thứ</span>
        </h1>
        
        <p className="text-lg md:text-xl text-gray-600 max-w-3xl mb-12 leading-relaxed">
          Cộng sự nghiên cứu và tư duy, dựa trên thông tin mà bạn tin cậy, được xây dựng bằng
          các mô hình ngôn ngữ mới nhất (Local & Gemini).
        </p>
        
        <button 
          onClick={() => navigate('/dashboard')}
          className="bg-black hover:bg-gray-800 text-white px-8 py-4 rounded-full text-lg font-medium transition-transform transform hover:scale-105 active:scale-95 shadow-xl"
        >
          Dùng thử NotebookLM
        </button>
      </main>

      {/* Footer Title */}
      <div className="pb-12 text-center">
        <h2 className="text-3xl font-normal text-gray-800">
          Cộng sự nghiên cứu bằng AI của bạn
        </h2>
      </div>

    </div>
  );
}

export default Landing;


# ==============================================================================

import { useState, useEffect, useRef, useMemo } from 'react';
import { useParams, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'motion/react';
import { spring, transition, blur } from '@animate-ui/react/utils/motion';
import { 
  Send, Bot, User, FileText, ChevronRight, Paperclip, Loader, Headphones, 
  RefreshCw, BookOpen, Brain, Layers, X, Check, XCircle, Trash2, 
  Link as LinkIcon, Plus, ArrowLeft, Settings, Cpu, Cloud, Award, Sparkles,
  Copy, Download, Eye, SlidersHorizontal, FileDown, Sun, Moon, Mic, MicOff,
  Search, HelpCircle, Network, Scale, Clock, Share2, Flame, ArrowUp, ArrowDown,
  Square, ShieldAlert, ClipboardCheck, Volume2, VolumeX, Bookmark, BookmarkPlus, Shield,
  PanelLeft, PanelLeftClose, PanelRight, PanelRightClose, ChevronDown, ChevronUp, LayoutGrid
} from 'lucide-react';
import SettingsModal from '../components/SettingsModal';
import QuizletModal from '../components/QuizletModal';
import MarkdownRenderer from '../components/MarkdownRenderer';
import MindmapModal from '../components/MindmapModal';
import DocCompareModal from '../components/DocCompareModal';
import TimelineOutlineModal from '../components/TimelineOutlineModal';
import ShortcutsModal from '../components/ShortcutsModal';
import ShareModal from '../components/ShareModal';
import ModelManagerModal from '../components/ModelManagerModal';

function Studio() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [activeNotebook, setActiveNotebook] = useState(null);
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState([]);
  const [input, setInput] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [studyGuide, setStudyGuide] = useState(null);
  const [regeneratingGuide, setRegeneratingGuide] = useState(false);
  const [documents, setDocuments] = useState([]);
  const [podcastGenerating, setPodcastGenerating] = useState(false);
  const [audioUrl, setAudioUrl] = useState(null);
  
  // Document selection for chat & generation context
  const [selectedDocs, setSelectedDocs] = useState(new Set());

  // Sidebar Visibility & Persistence
  const [showLeftSidebar, setShowLeftSidebar] = useState(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem('notebooklm_show_left_sidebar');
    return saved !== null ? saved === 'true' : true;
  });
  const [showRightSidebar, setShowRightSidebar] = useState(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem('notebooklm_show_right_sidebar');
    return saved !== null ? saved === 'true' : true;
  });

  // Studio tab state ('tools' | 'guide') & suggestions drawer toggle
  const [studioTab, setStudioTab] = useState('tools');
  const [showSuggestionsDrawer, setShowSuggestionsDrawer] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    localStorage.setItem('notebooklm_show_left_sidebar', String(showLeftSidebar));
  }, [showLeftSidebar]);

  useEffect(() => {
    localStorage.setItem('notebooklm_show_right_sidebar', String(showRightSidebar));
  }, [showRightSidebar]);

  // Settings & Model Switching State
  const [showSettings, setShowSettings] = useState(false);
  const [showModelPicker, setShowModelPicker] = useState(false);
  const [availableLocalModels, setAvailableLocalModels] = useState([]);
  const [showModelManagerModal, setShowModelManagerModal] = useState(false);
  const [modelManagerInitialTab, setModelManagerInitialTab] = useState('recommend');
  const [customSavedModels, setCustomSavedModels] = useState([]);

  // Quiz & Flashcard state
  const [showQuiz, setShowQuiz] = useState(false);
  const [showFlashcards, setShowFlashcards] = useState(false);
  const [currentFlashcard, setCurrentFlashcard] = useState(0);
  const [flashcardFlipped, setFlashcardFlipped] = useState(false);
  const [quizAnswers, setQuizAnswers] = useState({});
  const [quizSubmitted, setQuizSubmitted] = useState(false);

  // Evaluation state
  const [evaluating, setEvaluating] = useState(false);
  const [evalResult, setEvalResult] = useState(null);
  const [showEvalModal, setShowEvalModal] = useState(false);

  const [showUrlInput, setShowUrlInput] = useState(false);
  const [urlInputValue, setUrlInputValue] = useState('');
  const [uploadingDocs, setUploadingDocs] = useState([]);

  // Paste Raw Text Modal state
  const [showPasteTextModal, setShowPasteTextModal] = useState(false);
  const [pasteTitle, setPasteTitle] = useState('');
  const [pasteContent, setPasteContent] = useState('');

  // Custom Quizlet & Study Guide generation state
  const [showGuideConfigModal, setShowGuideConfigModal] = useState(false);
  const [guideConfig, setGuideConfig] = useState({
    quiz_count: 5,
    flashcards_count: 5,
    difficulty: 'medium',
    focus_topic: ''
  });

  // Custom Podcast state
  const [showPodcastModal, setShowPodcastModal] = useState(false);
  const [podcastConfig, setPodcastConfig] = useState({
    focus_topic: '',
    style: 'fun',
    voice_mode: 'duo'
  });

  // Document preview state
  const [previewDoc, setPreviewDoc] = useState(null);
  const [loadingPreview, setLoadingPreview] = useState(false);

  // Message copy state
  const [copiedMsgIdx, setCopiedMsgIdx] = useState(null);

  // Theme state ('dark' | 'light')
  const [theme, setTheme] = useState(() => localStorage.getItem('notebooklm_theme') || 'dark');
  const toggleTheme = () => {
    const nextTheme = theme === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark';
    setTheme(nextTheme);
    localStorage.setItem('notebooklm_theme', nextTheme);
  };
  const isDark = theme === 'dark';

  // Mobile navigation tab ('docs' | 'chat' | 'studio')
  const [mobileActiveTab, setMobileActiveTab] = useState('chat');

  // Chat Search state
  const [showChatSearch, setShowChatSearch] = useState(false);
  const [chatSearchQuery, setChatSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [currentMatchIdx, setCurrentMatchIdx] = useState(0);

  // Chat Persona mode ('strict' | 'socratic' | 'critic')
  const [chatPersona, setChatPersona] = useState('strict');

  const PERSONA_CONFIGS = {
    strict: {
      id: 'strict',
      name: 'Bám sát nguồn',
      desc: 'Trả lời chính xác và chỉ dựa vào tài liệu được cung cấp, không suy diễn ngoài lề.',
      icon: Shield,
      prefix: '[Chế độ: Bám sát nguồn] Trả lời chính xác và chỉ dựa vào tài liệu được cung cấp, không suy diễn ngoài lề.\n\n'
    },
    socratic: {
      id: 'socratic',
      name: 'Gia sư Socratic',
      desc: 'Đặt câu hỏi gợi mở, hướng dẫn tư duy từng bước.',
      icon: Sparkles,
      prefix: '[Chế độ: Gia sư Socratic] Hãy đóng vai một gia sư Socratic thông thái. Đặt câu hỏi gợi mở, phân tích tư duy từng bước và giúp người học tự khám phá câu trả lời dựa trên tài liệu.\n\n'
    },
    critic: {
      id: 'critic',
      name: 'Phản biện sâu',
      desc: 'Phân tích đa chiều, chỉ ra các giả định, điểm mạnh và hạn chế trong tài liệu.',
      icon: Flame,
      prefix: '[Chế độ: Phản biện sâu] Hãy đóng vai chuyên gia phản biện học thuật sắc bén. Đánh giá đa chiều, chỉ ra các giả định ngầm, điểm mạnh và hạn chế trong tài liệu đối với câu hỏi sau.\n\n'
    }
  };

  // TTS per message state & methods
  const [speakingMsgIdx, setSpeakingMsgIdx] = useState(null);

  const cleanTextForSpeech = (rawText) => {
    if (!rawText) return '';
    return rawText
      .replace(/\[\^?\d+\]/g, '')
      .replace(/```[\s\S]*?```/g, ' Đoạn mã. ')
      .replace(/`([^`]+)`/g, '$1')
      .replace(/[*_~#>]/g, '')
      .replace(/\[([^\]]+)\]\([^)]+\)/g, '$1')
      .replace(/\n+/g, '. ')
      .trim();
  };

  const handleSpeakMessage = (text, idx) => {
    if (!('speechSynthesis' in window)) {
      alert('Trình duyệt của bạn không hỗ trợ tính năng Web Speech Text-to-Speech.');
      return;
    }

    if (speakingMsgIdx === idx) {
      window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
      setSpeakingMsgIdx(null);
      return;
    }

    window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
    const cleanText = cleanTextForSpeech(text);
    if (!cleanText) return;

    const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(cleanText);
    utterance.lang = 'vi-VN';
    utterance.rate = 1.0;
    utterance.pitch = 1.0;

    utterance.onend = () => {
      setSpeakingMsgIdx(null);
    };
    utterance.onerror = () => {
      setSpeakingMsgIdx(null);
    };

    setSpeakingMsgIdx(idx);
    window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
  };

  // Pinned Notes (Bảng Ghi chú Nghiên cứu) State
  const [pinnedNotes, setPinnedNotes] = useState([]);
  const [showNotesModal, setShowNotesModal] = useState(false);
  const [newNoteTitle, setNewNoteTitle] = useState('');
  const [newNoteContent, setNewNoteContent] = useState('');
  const [savingNote, setSavingNote] = useState(false);
  const [isSynthesizingNotes, setIsSynthesizingNotes] = useState(false);
  const [convertingNoteId, setConvertingNoteId] = useState(null);

  // Toast Notification State
  const [toastMsg, setToastMsg] = useState(null);
  const toastTimeoutRef = useRef(null);

  const showToast = (msg, duration = 3000) => {
    if (toastTimeoutRef.current) clearTimeout(toastTimeoutRef.current);
    setToastMsg(msg);
    toastTimeoutRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
      setToastMsg(null);
    }, duration);
  };

  const fetchPinnedNotes = async (nbId) => {
    if (!nbId) return;
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/notebooks/${nbId}/notes`);
      if (res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json();
        setPinnedNotes(data.notes || []);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Lỗi lấy ghi chú ghim:", err);
    }
  };

  const handlePinNote = async (content, title = "Ghi chú", source = "manual") => {
    const targetNbId = activeNotebook?.id || id;
    if (!content || !targetNbId) return;
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/notebooks/${targetNbId}/notes`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ content, title, source })
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json();
        setPinnedNotes(data.notes || []);
        showToast("📌 Đã ghim vào Bảng Ghi chú!");
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Lỗi khi ghim ghi chú:", err);
      showToast("❌ Không thể ghim ghi chú: " + err.message);
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteNote = async (noteId) => {
    const targetNbId = activeNotebook?.id || id;
    if (!targetNbId || !noteId) return;
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/notebooks/${targetNbId}/notes/${noteId}`, {
        method: 'DELETE'
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json();
        setPinnedNotes(data.notes || []);
        showToast("🗑️ Đã xóa ghi chú");
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Lỗi xóa ghi chú:", err);
    }
  };

  const handleSynthesizeNotes = async () => {
    const targetNbId = activeNotebook?.id || id;
    if (!targetNbId || pinnedNotes.length < 2) return;
    setIsSynthesizingNotes(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/notebooks/${targetNbId}/notes/synthesize`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          'x-notebook-id': targetNbId
        }
      });
      if (!res.ok) {
        const err = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
        throw new Error(err.detail || 'Không thể tổng hợp ghi chú');
      }
      const data = await res.json();
      setCritiqueModal({
        isOpen: true,
        type: 'synthesis',
        title: data.title || 'Báo cáo Tổng hợp Ghi chú Nghiên cứu',
        content: data.markdown || '',
        loading: false
      });
      setShowNotesModal(false);
      showToast('✨ Đã tổng hợp Báo cáo Chuyên đề thành công!');
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Lỗi tổng hợp ghi chú:", err);
      showToast(`❌ Lỗi: ${err.message || 'Không thể tổng hợp ghi chú'}`);
    } finally {
      setIsSynthesizingNotes(false);
    }
  };

  const handleConvertNoteToSource = async (note) => {
    const targetNbId = activeNotebook?.id || id;
    if (!targetNbId || !note) return;
    setConvertingNoteId(note.id);
    try {
      const rawTitle = (note.title || 'Ghi chú').trim();
      const docTitle = rawTitle.endsWith('.md') || rawTitle.endsWith('.txt') ? rawTitle : `${rawTitle}.md`;
      const res = await fetch('/api/ingest/text', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          'x-notebook-id': targetNbId
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          notebook_id: targetNbId,
          title: docTitle,
          text: note.content
        })
      });
      if (!res.ok) {
        const err = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
        throw new Error(err.detail || 'Không thể nạp ghi chú thành nguồn');
      }
      await fetchDocuments();
      setSelectedDocs(prev => new Set([...prev, docTitle]));
      showToast(`✅ Đã nạp "${docTitle}" thành tài liệu nguồn RAG!`);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Lỗi chuyển ghi chú thành nguồn:", err);
      showToast(`❌ Lỗi: ${err.message || 'Không thể chuyển ghi chú'}`);
    } finally {
      setConvertingNoteId(null);
    }
  };

  // Chat Search match indices & jump handlers
  const matchedIndices = useMemo(() => {
    if (!chatSearchQuery.trim()) return [];
    const q = chatSearchQuery.toLowerCase();
    const indices = [];
    messages.forEach((m, idx) => {
      if (m.content && m.content.toLowerCase().includes(q)) {
        indices.push(idx);
      }
    });
    return indices;
  }, [messages, chatSearchQuery]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setCurrentMatchIdx(0);
  }, [chatSearchQuery]);

  const scrollToMatch = (matchIdx) => {
    if (matchedIndices.length === 0) return;
    const targetMsgIdx = matchedIndices[matchIdx];
    const el = document.getElementById(`msg-${targetMsgIdx}`);
    if (el) {
      el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
    }
  };

  const handleNextMatch = () => {
    if (matchedIndices.length === 0) return;
    const nextIdx = (currentMatchIdx + 1) % matchedIndices.length;
    setCurrentMatchIdx(nextIdx);
    scrollToMatch(nextIdx);
  };

  const handlePrevMatch = () => {
    if (matchedIndices.length === 0) return;
    const prevIdx = (currentMatchIdx - 1 + matchedIndices.length) % matchedIndices.length;
    setCurrentMatchIdx(prevIdx);
    scrollToMatch(prevIdx);
  };

  // Voice recording state
  const [isRecording, setIsRecording] = useState(false);
  const recognitionRef = useRef(null);
  const voiceBaseTextRef = useRef('');
  const chatInputRef = useRef(null);
  const abortControllerRef = useRef(null);

  // Mindmap state
  const [showMindmapModal, setShowMindmapModal] = useState(false);
  const [mindmapData, setMindmapData] = useState(null);
  const [loadingMindmap, setLoadingMindmap] = useState(false);

  // Document comparison state
  const [showDocCompareModal, setShowDocCompareModal] = useState(false);
  const [comparisonResult, setComparisonResult] = useState('');
  const [comparingDocs, setComparingDocs] = useState(false);

  // Timeline & Outline state
  const [showTimelineModal, setShowTimelineModal] = useState(false);
  const [timelineData, setTimelineData] = useState(null);
  const [loadingTimeline, setLoadingTimeline] = useState(false);

  // Shortcuts & Share modals
  const [showShortcutsModal, setShowShortcutsModal] = useState(false);
  const [showShareModal, setShowShareModal] = useState(false);

  // Citation highlight snippet in preview
  const [highlightSnippet, setHighlightSnippet] = useState(null);

  // Source Inspector Modal State (Chunks viewer)
  const [inspectDoc, setInspectDoc] = useState(null);
  const [docChunks, setDocChunks] = useState([]);
  const [loadingChunks, setLoadingChunks] = useState(false);
  const [chunkSearch, setChunkSearch] = useState('');

  // Critique & Action Plan State
  const [critiqueModal, setCritiqueModal] = useState({ isOpen: false, type: '', title: '', content: '', loading: false });

  // Tools definition for Studio Analysis
  const tools = [
    { id: 'critique', name: 'Phản biện AI', desc: 'Tìm lỗ hổng & góc nhìn phản biện', icon: ShieldAlert, endpoint: '/api/studio/critique', color: 'text-rose-600', bg: 'bg-rose-50' },
    { id: 'action_plan', name: 'Kế hoạch hành động', desc: 'Action Matrix & Checklist thực thi', icon: ClipboardCheck, endpoint: '/api/studio/action-plan', color: 'text-lime-600', bg: 'bg-lime-50' },
  ];

  // Suggested questions extracted from studyGuide.faq
  const suggestedQuestions = useMemo(() => {
    if (!studyGuide?.faq) return [];
    const lines = studyGuide.faq.split('\n');
    const questions = [];
    for (const line of lines) {
      let clean = line.trim()
        .replace(/^#+\s*/, '')
        .replace(/^\*+\s*/, '')
        .replace(/^-\s*/, '')
        .replace(/^[0-9]+[.)]\s*/, '')
        .replace(/\*+/g, '')
        .trim();
      clean = clean.replace(/^[0-9]+[.)]\s*/, '').trim();
      if (clean.endsWith('?') && clean.length > 8 && clean.length < 140) {
        questions.push(clean);
      }
    }
    return questions.slice(0, 4);
  }, [studyGuide?.faq]);

  const messagesEndRef = useRef(null);

  // Global Keyboard Shortcuts
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e) => {
      // Escape closes open modals or stops generation
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        if (loading) {
          handleStopGenerating();
          return;
        }
        setShowModelPicker(false);
        setShowChatSearch(false);
        setShowSettings(false);
        setShowQuiz(false);
        setShowFlashcards(false);
        setShowEvalModal(false);
        setShowGuideConfigModal(false);
        setShowPodcastModal(false);
        setPreviewDoc(null);
        setShowMindmapModal(false);
        setShowDocCompareModal(false);
        setShowTimelineModal(false);
        setShowShortcutsModal(false);
        setShowShareModal(false);
        setShowModelManagerModal(false);
        setShowPasteTextModal(false);
        setShowNotesModal(false);
        setInspectDoc(null);
        setCritiqueModal(prev => ({ ...prev, isOpen: false }));
        if ('speechSynthesis' in window) {
          window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
          setSpeakingMsgIdx(null);
        }
        return;
      }

      // Check for Cmd/Ctrl modifier
      const isCmdOrCtrl = e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey;
      if (!isCmdOrCtrl) return;

      if (e.key === 'b' || e.key === 'B') {
        e.preventDefault();
        setShowLeftSidebar(prev => !prev);
      } else if (e.key === 'j' || e.key === 'J') {
        e.preventDefault();
        setShowRightSidebar(prev => !prev);
      } else if (e.key === 'k' || e.key === 'K') {
        e.preventDefault();
        setShowModelPicker(prev => !prev);
      } else if (e.key === 'f' || e.key === 'F') {
        e.preventDefault();
        setShowChatSearch(prev => !prev);
      } else if (e.key === '/') {
        e.preventDefault();
        chatInputRef.current?.focus();
      } else if (e.shiftKey && (e.key === 's' || e.key === 'S')) {
        e.preventDefault();
        setShowSettings(prev => !prev);
      } else if (e.shiftKey && (e.key === 'q' || e.key === 'Q')) {
        e.preventDefault();
        setShowFlashcards(prev => !prev);
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, []);

  // Auto scroll
  const scrollToBottom = () => {
    messagesEndRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth" });
  };
  useEffect(() => {
    scrollToBottom();
  }, [messages]);

  // Fetch data khi mount
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!id) return;
    fetchNotebookDetails();
    fetchLocalModels();
  }, [id]);

  const fetchNotebookDetails = async () => {
    try {
      const nbRes = await fetch(`/api/notebooks/${id}`);
      if (!nbRes.ok) throw new Error("Không tìm thấy sổ tay");
      const nb = await nbRes.json();
      setActiveNotebook(nb);
      
      const msgRes = await fetch(`/api/notebooks/${nb.id}/messages`);
      const msgs = await msgRes.json();
      if (msgs && msgs.length > 0) {
        setMessages(msgs);
      } else {
        const modelLabel = nb.model_name ? ` (${nb.provider?.toUpperCase()}: ${nb.model_name})` : '';
        setMessages([{ 
          role: 'assistant', 
          content: `Xin chào! Bạn đang ở sổ tay "${nb.title}"${modelLabel}. Hãy tải tài liệu hoặc gửi link bài viết/YouTube để bắt đầu hỏi đáp nhé!`, 
          citations: [] 
        }]);
      }

      const guideRes = await fetch(`/api/notebooks/${nb.id}/study-guide`);
      if (guideRes.ok) {
        const guide = await guideRes.json();
        setStudyGuide(guide);
      }

      const docRes = await fetch(`/api/notebooks/${nb.id}/documents`);
      if (docRes.ok) {
        const docs = await docRes.json();
        setDocuments(docs);
        setSelectedDocs(new Set(docs.map(d => d.filename)));
      }
      fetchPinnedNotes(nb.id);
    } catch (e) {
      console.error(e);
    }
  };

  const fetchLocalModels = async () => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/llm/local-models');
      const data = await res.json();
      setAvailableLocalModels(data?.ollama?.models || []);
      
      const storedCustom = localStorage.getItem('custom_ai_models');
      if (storedCustom) {
        setCustomSavedModels(JSON.parse(storedCustom));
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("Lỗi lấy local models:", e);
    }
  };

  // Switch model on the fly for active notebook
  const handleSwitchModel = async (newProvider, newModelName, newBaseUrl, newApiKey) => {
    setShowModelPicker(false);
    try {
      const payload = {
        provider: newProvider,
        model_name: newModelName,
        is_private: ['ollama', 'lm_studio', 'local_ai'].includes(newProvider)
      };
      if (newBaseUrl !== undefined) payload.base_url = newBaseUrl;
      if (newApiKey !== undefined) payload.api_key = newApiKey;

      const res = await fetch(`/api/notebooks/${activeNotebook.id}/settings`, {
        method: 'PUT',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(payload)
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        const updated = await res.json();
        setActiveNotebook(updated.notebook);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("Lỗi đổi model:", e);
    }
  };

  // Quản lý việc chọn/bỏ chọn tài liệu làm ngữ cảnh
  const toggleSelectDoc = (filename) => {
    setSelectedDocs(prev => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      if (next.has(filename)) {
        next.delete(filename);
      } else {
        next.add(filename);
      }
      return next;
    });
  };

  const handleSelectAllDocs = () => {
    setSelectedDocs(new Set(documents.map(d => d.filename)));
  };

  const handleDeselectAllDocs = () => {
    setSelectedDocs(new Set());
  };

  // Polling Study Guide if it is null AND there are documents
  useEffect(() => {
    let intervalId;
    if (activeNotebook && !studyGuide && documents.length > 0) {
      intervalId = setInterval(async () => {
        try {
          const guideRes = await fetch(`/api/notebooks/${activeNotebook.id}/study-guide`);
          if (guideRes.ok) {
            const guide = await guideRes.json();
            if (guide) {
              setStudyGuide(guide);
              clearInterval(intervalId);
            }
          }
        } catch (e) {
          console.error("Lỗi khi poll Study Guide:", e);
        }
      }, 3000);
    }
    return () => {
      if (intervalId) clearInterval(intervalId);
    };
  }, [activeNotebook, studyGuide, documents]);

  const handleRegenerateStudyGuide = async () => {
    if (!activeNotebook || regeneratingGuide) return;
    setRegeneratingGuide(true);
    try {
      await fetch(`/api/notebooks/${activeNotebook.id}/study-guide/regenerate`, { 
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          ...guideConfig,
          selected_documents: selectedDocs.size > 0 ? Array.from(selectedDocs) : (documents || []).map(d => d.filename)
        })
      });
      setStudyGuide(null); // Kích hoạt polling
    } catch (e) {
      alert("Lỗi khi yêu cầu tạo lại cẩm nang: " + e.message);
    } finally {
      setTimeout(() => setRegeneratingGuide(false), 2000);
    }
  };

  const handleGenerateCustomGuide = async () => {
    if (!activeNotebook || regeneratingGuide) return;
    setShowGuideConfigModal(false);
    setShowQuiz(false);
    setShowFlashcards(false);
    setRegeneratingGuide(true);
    try {
      await fetch(`/api/notebooks/${activeNotebook.id}/study-guide/regenerate`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          ...guideConfig,
          selected_documents: selectedDocs.size > 0 ? Array.from(selectedDocs) : (documents || []).map(d => d.filename)
        })
      });
      setStudyGuide(null); // Kích hoạt polling cập nhật giao diện
    } catch (e) {
      alert("Lỗi khi tạo lại cẩm nang: " + e.message);
    } finally {
      setTimeout(() => setRegeneratingGuide(false), 2000);
    }
  };

  const generatePodcast = async () => {
    if (!activeNotebook) return;
    setPodcastGenerating(true);
    setShowPodcastModal(false);
    setAudioUrl(null);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/podcast/generate', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ 
          notebook_id: activeNotebook.id,
          focus_topic: podcastConfig.focus_topic,
          style: podcastConfig.style,
          voice_mode: podcastConfig.voice_mode || 'duo',
          selected_documents: selectedDocs.size > 0 ? Array.from(selectedDocs) : (documents || []).map(d => d.filename)
        })
      });
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error("Lỗi khi gọi API tạo Podcast");
      
      // Polling kiểm tra tiến trình Podcast mỗi 2s (tối đa 60 lần ~ 120 giây)
      let attempts = 0;
      const maxAttempts = 60;
      const pollAudio = setInterval(async () => {
        attempts += 1;
        try {
          // 1. Kiểm tra status endpoint
          const statusRes = await fetch(`/api/podcast/status/${activeNotebook.id}`);
          if (statusRes.ok) {
            const statusData = await statusRes.json();
            if (statusData.has_audio || statusData.status === 'completed') {
              clearInterval(pollAudio);
              setAudioUrl(`/api/podcast/audio/${activeNotebook.id}?t=${Date.now()}`);
              setPodcastGenerating(false);
              showToast("🎉 Podcast đã được tạo thành công!");
              return;
            }
            if (statusData.status === 'error') {
              clearInterval(pollAudio);
              setPodcastGenerating(false);
              showToast(`❌ Lỗi tạo Podcast: ${statusData.error || 'Thất bại'}`);
              return;
            }
          }

          // 2. Dự phòng kiểm tra trực tiếp file audio
          const checkRes = await fetch(`/api/podcast/audio/${activeNotebook.id}`);
          if (checkRes.ok && checkRes.status === 200) {
            clearInterval(pollAudio);
            setAudioUrl(`/api/podcast/audio/${activeNotebook.id}?t=${Date.now()}`);
            setPodcastGenerating(false);
            showToast("🎉 Podcast đã được tạo thành công!");
            return;
          }

          if (attempts >= maxAttempts) {
            clearInterval(pollAudio);
            setPodcastGenerating(false);
            showToast("⚠️ Quá thời gian tạo Podcast. Vui lòng kiểm tra lại kết nối mô hình AI và thử lại.", 5000);
          }
        } catch (pollErr) {
          if (attempts >= maxAttempts) {
            clearInterval(pollAudio);
            setPodcastGenerating(false);
            showToast("⚠️ Quá thời gian tạo Podcast. Vui lòng thử lại.", 5000);
          }
        }
      }, 2000);
    } catch (e) {
      console.error(e);
      showToast("❌ Lỗi tạo podcast: " + e.message);
      setPodcastGenerating(false);
    }
  };

  const handleCopyMessage = (content, idx) => {
    if (!content) return;
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(content);
    setCopiedMsgIdx(idx);
    setTimeout(() => setCopiedMsgIdx(null), 2000);
  };

  const handleExportChat = () => {
    if (!messages || messages.length === 0) {
      alert("Chưa có tin nhắn nào để xuất!");
      return;
    }
    let md = `# Sổ tay: ${activeNotebook?.title || 'NotebookLM'}\n\n`;
    md += `*Thời gian xuất: ${new Date().toLocaleString('vi-VN')}*\n`;
    md += `*Mô hình: ${currentProvider?.toUpperCase()}: ${currentModel}*\n\n`;
    md += `---\n\n`;

    messages.forEach((msg) => {
      const roleName = msg.role === 'user' ? '👤 **Người dùng:**' : '🤖 **Trợ lý AI:**';
      md += `${roleName}\n\n${msg.content || ''}\n\n`;
      if (msg.citations && msg.citations.length > 0) {
        md += `> **Trích dẫn nguồn:**\n`;
        msg.citations.forEach((c) => {
          md += `> - [${c.marker}] ${c.filename}\n`;
        });
        md += `\n`;
      }
      md += `---\n\n`;
    });

    const blob = new Blob([md], { type: 'text/markdown;charset=utf-8' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    const safeTitle = (activeNotebook?.title || 'cuoc_tro_chuyen').replace(/[\s/\\?%*:|"<>]/g, '_');
    a.download = `${safeTitle}_chat_history.md`;
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  // --- Feature #5: Message Delete & Clear ---
  const handleDeleteMessage = async (msgId, index) => {
    if (msgId) {
      try {
        await fetch(`/api/notebooks/${activeNotebook.id}/messages/${msgId}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
      } catch (e) {
        console.error("Lỗi xóa tin nhắn:", e);
      }
    }
    setMessages(prev => prev.filter((_, i) => i !== index));
  };

  const handleClearAllMessages = async () => {
    if (!window.confirm("Bạn có chắc chắn muốn xóa toàn bộ lịch sử trò chuyện của sổ tay này?")) return;
    try {
      await fetch(`/api/notebooks/${activeNotebook.id}/messages`, { method: 'DELETE' });
      setMessages([]);
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("Lỗi xóa toàn bộ hội thoại:", e);
    }
  };

  // --- Feature #10: Voice Input via Web Speech API ---
  const handleVoiceToggle = () => {
    const SpeechRecognition = window.SpeechRecognition || window.webkitSpeechRecognition;
    if (!SpeechRecognition) {
      alert("Trình duyệt hiện tại chưa hỗ trợ nhận diện giọng nói trực tiếp. Vui lòng dùng Chrome, Edge hoặc Cốc Cốc.");
      return;
    }

    if (isRecording) {
      if (recognitionRef.current) {
        try {
          recognitionRef.current.stop();
        } catch (_) {}
      }
      setIsRecording(false);
      return;
    }

    try {
      const recognition = new SpeechRecognition();
      recognition.lang = 'vi-VN';
      recognition.continuous = false;
      recognition.interimResults = true;

      // Lưu lại văn bản hiện có trước khi ghi âm để không bị ghi đè hoặc lặp
      voiceBaseTextRef.current = input.trim();

      recognition.onstart = () => setIsRecording(true);
      recognition.onresult = (event) => {
        let finalStr = '';
        let interimStr = '';
        for (let i = 0; i < event.results.length; ++i) {
          const item = event.results[i];
          if (item.isFinal) {
            finalStr += item[0]?.transcript || '';
          } else {
            interimStr += item[0]?.transcript || '';
          }
        }
        const spoken = (finalStr + interimStr).trim();
        if (spoken) {
          const base = voiceBaseTextRef.current;
          setInput(base ? `${base} ${spoken}` : spoken);
        }
      };
      recognition.onerror = (e) => {
        console.error("Lỗi nhận diện âm thanh:", e);
        setIsRecording(false);
      };
      recognition.onend = () => setIsRecording(false);

      recognitionRef.current = recognition;
      recognition.start();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      setIsRecording(false);
    }
  };

  // --- Feature #13: Click Citation to Highlight in Preview ---
  const handleCitationClick = async (cit) => {
    setLoadingPreview(true);
    setHighlightSnippet(cit.snippet || null);
    setPreviewDoc({ filename: cit.filename, loading: true });
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/notebooks/${activeNotebook.id}/documents/preview?filename=${encodeURIComponent(cit.filename)}`);
      if (res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json();
        setPreviewDoc(data);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.error(e);
    } finally {
      setLoadingPreview(false);
    }
  };

  // --- Feature #8: Mindmap Handlers ---
  const handleOpenMindmap = async () => {
    setShowMindmapModal(true);
    if (!mindmapData) {
      setLoadingMindmap(true);
      try {
        const res = await fetch(`/api/notebooks/${activeNotebook.id}/mindmap`);
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.mindmap) {
          setMindmapData(data.mindmap);
        } else {
          await handleRegenerateMindmap();
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error(e);
      } finally {
        setLoadingMindmap(false);
      }
    }
  };

  const handleRegenerateMindmap = async (topic = "") => {
    setLoadingMindmap(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/notebooks/${activeNotebook.id}/mindmap/generate`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ 
          focus_topic: topic,
          selected_documents: selectedDocs.size > 0 ? Array.from(selectedDocs) : (documents || []).map(d => d.filename)
        })
      });
      if (!res.ok) {
        const errJson = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
        throw new Error(errJson.detail || `Lỗi máy chủ (${res.status})`);
      }
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.mindmap) {
        setMindmapData(data.mindmap);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      alert("Lỗi tạo sơ đồ tư duy: " + e.message);
    } finally {
      setLoadingMindmap(false);
    }
  };

  // --- Feature #15: Timeline & Outline Handlers ---
  const handleOpenTimeline = async () => {
    setShowTimelineModal(true);
    if (!timelineData) {
      setLoadingTimeline(true);
      try {
        const res = await fetch(`/api/notebooks/${activeNotebook.id}/timeline-outline`);
        if (res.ok) {
          const data = await res.json();
          if (data.timeline_outline) {
            setTimelineData(data.timeline_outline);
          } else {
            await handleRegenerateTimeline();
          }
        } else {
          await handleRegenerateTimeline();
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error(e);
      } finally {
        setLoadingTimeline(false);
      }
    }
  };

  const handleRegenerateTimeline = async (topic = "") => {
    setLoadingTimeline(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/notebooks/${activeNotebook.id}/timeline-outline/generate`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ 
          focus_topic: topic,
          selected_documents: selectedDocs.size > 0 ? Array.from(selectedDocs) : (documents || []).map(d => d.filename)
        })
      });
      if (!res.ok) {
        const errJson = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
        throw new Error(errJson.detail || `Lỗi máy chủ (${res.status})`);
      }
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.timeline_outline) {
        setTimelineData(data.timeline_outline);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      alert("Lỗi trích xuất dòng thời gian & dàn ý: " + e.message);
    } finally {
      setLoadingTimeline(false);
    }
  };

  // --- Feature #9: Document Comparison Handler ---
  const handleCompareDocs = async (selectedDocList, topic = "") => {
    setComparingDocs(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/notebooks/${activeNotebook.id}/documents/compare`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ filenames: selectedDocList, focus_topic: topic })
      });
      if (!res.ok) {
        const errJson = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
        throw new Error(errJson.detail || `Lỗi máy chủ (${res.status})`);
      }
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.comparison) {
        setComparisonResult(data.comparison);
      } else {
        alert("Không nhận được kết quả so sánh.");
      }
    } catch (e) {
      alert("Lỗi khi so sánh tài liệu: " + e.message);
    } finally {
      setComparingDocs(false);
    }
  };


  // --- Feature #12: Export Notebook JSON ---
  const handleExportNotebookJson = async () => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/notebooks/${activeNotebook.id}/export`);
      const data = await res.json();
      const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(data, null, 2)], { type: 'application/json' });
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const a = document.createElement('a');
      a.href = url;
      a.download = `notebook-${(activeNotebook.title || 'backup').replace(/[\s/\\?%*:|"<>]/g, '_')}-${Date.now()}.json`;
      a.click();
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    } catch (e) {
      alert("Lỗi xuất dữ liệu: " + e.message);
    }
  };

  const handlePreviewDocument = async (filename) => {
    setHighlightSnippet(null);
    setLoadingPreview(true);
    setPreviewDoc({ filename, loading: true });
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/notebooks/${activeNotebook.id}/documents/preview?filename=${encodeURIComponent(filename)}`);
      if (res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json();
        setPreviewDoc(data);
      } else {
        setPreviewDoc({ filename, preview_text: "Không thể lấy nội dung xem trước của tài liệu này." });
      }
    } catch (e) {
      setPreviewDoc({ filename, preview_text: "Lỗi kết nối khi xem trước tài liệu: " + e.message });
    } finally {
      setLoadingPreview(false);
    }
  };

  const handleInspectChunks = async (sourceName, e) => {
    if (e) e.stopPropagation();
    setInspectDoc(sourceName);
    setLoadingChunks(true);
    setChunkSearch('');
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/documents/chunks?source=${encodeURIComponent(sourceName)}&notebook_id=${activeNotebook?.id || ''}`);
      if (res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json();
        setDocChunks(data.chunks || []);
      } else {
        setDocChunks([]);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Lỗi tải chunks:", err);
      setDocChunks([]);
    } finally {
      setLoadingChunks(false);
    }
  };

  const handleRunStudioTool = async (toolId) => {
    const isCritique = toolId === 'critique';
    const nbId = activeNotebook?.id || id || '';
    const endpoint = isCritique 
      ? `/api/studio/critique?notebook_id=${encodeURIComponent(nbId)}` 
      : `/api/studio/action-plan?notebook_id=${encodeURIComponent(nbId)}`;
    const title = isCritique ? 'Báo Cáo Phản Biện & Tìm Lỗ Hổng (Peer Review)' : 'Kế Hoạch Hành Động Thực Tiễn (Action Plan)';
    
    setCritiqueModal({ isOpen: true, type: toolId, title, content: '', loading: true });
    
    try {
      const res = await fetch(endpoint, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          'x-notebook-id': nbId
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          notebook_id: nbId,
          source_filter: selectedDocs.size === 1 ? Array.from(selectedDocs)[0] : null,
          selected_documents: selectedDocs.size > 0 ? Array.from(selectedDocs) : (documents || []).map(d => d.filename)
        })
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json();
        setCritiqueModal({ isOpen: true, type: toolId, title, content: data.content || data.error || 'Không có kết quả phân tích.', loading: false });
      } else {
        const err = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
        setCritiqueModal({ isOpen: true, type: toolId, title, content: err.detail || 'Lỗi khi gọi API phân tích.', loading: false });
      }
    } catch (e) {
      setCritiqueModal({ isOpen: true, type: toolId, title, content: 'Lỗi kết nối máy chủ: ' + e.message, loading: false });
    }
  };

  const deleteNotebook = async () => {
    if (!activeNotebook) return;
    if (!window.confirm("Bạn có chắc chắn muốn xóa sổ tay này và toàn bộ dữ liệu bên trong không?")) return;
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/notebooks/${activeNotebook.id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
      if (res.ok) {
        navigate('/dashboard');
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.error(e);
    }
  };

  const handleUrlSubmit = async () => {
    if (!urlInputValue.trim() || !activeNotebook) return;
    
    let urlToSubmit = urlInputValue.trim();
    if (!urlToSubmit.startsWith('http://') && !urlToSubmit.startsWith('https://')) {
      urlToSubmit = 'https://' + urlToSubmit;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    setShowUrlInput(false);
    setUploadingDocs(prev => [...prev, { filename: urlToSubmit }]);
    
    try {
      const formData = new FormData();
      formData.append('url', urlToSubmit);
      formData.append('notebook_id', activeNotebook.id);

      const res = await fetch('/api/ingest/url', {
        method: 'POST',
        body: formData
      });
      
      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Mã lỗi từ máy chủ: ${res.status}`);
      }

      await res.json();
      setUrlInputValue('');
      
      const docRes = await fetch(`/api/notebooks/${activeNotebook.id}/documents`);
      if (docRes.ok) {
        const newDocs = await docRes.json();
        setDocuments(newDocs);
        setSelectedDocs(new Set(newDocs.map(d => d.filename)));
      }
    } catch (e) {
      setMessages(prev => [...prev, { role: 'assistant', content: `Lỗi khi nạp link: ${e.message}`, citations: [] }]);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
      setUploadingDocs(prev => prev.filter(d => d.filename !== urlToSubmit));
    }
  };

  // Hỗ trợ tải lên NHIỀU FILE cùng lúc (Multi-file Upload)
  const handleFileUpload = async (e) => {
    if (!activeNotebook) return;
    const files = Array.from(e.target.files || []);
    if (files.length === 0) return;

    setUploading(true);
    setUploadingDocs(prev => [...prev, ...files.map(f => ({ filename: f.name }))]);

    for (const file of files) {
      const formData = new FormData();
      formData.append('file', file);
      formData.append('notebook_id', activeNotebook.id);

      try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/ingest/upload', {
          method: 'POST',
          body: formData,
        });
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Upload failed: ${file.name}`);
        await response.json();
        setSelectedDocs(prev => new Set([...prev, file.name]));
      } catch (err) {
        console.error(err);
        setMessages(prev => [...prev, { role: 'assistant', content: `❌ Lỗi khi tải tệp "${file.name}": ${err.message}`, citations: [] }]);
      } finally {
        setUploadingDocs(prev => prev.filter(d => d.filename !== file.name));
      }
    }

    try {
      const docRes = await fetch(`/api/notebooks/${activeNotebook.id}/documents`);
      if (docRes.ok) {
        const newDocs = await docRes.json();
        setDocuments(newDocs);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
      e.target.value = null;
    }
  };

  // Nạp văn bản dán trực tiếp (Paste Text)
  const handlePasteTextSubmit = async () => {
    if (!pasteContent.trim() || !activeNotebook) return;
    const tempTitle = pasteTitle.trim() || `Ghi chú (${new Date().toLocaleTimeString('vi-VN')}).txt`;
    setShowPasteTextModal(false);
    setUploading(true);
    setUploadingDocs(prev => [...prev, { filename: tempTitle }]);

    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/ingest/text', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          notebook_id: activeNotebook.id,
          title: tempTitle,
          text: pasteContent
        })
      });
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Mã lỗi: ${res.status}`);
      const data = await res.json();
      setPasteTitle('');
      setPasteContent('');

      const docRes = await fetch(`/api/notebooks/${activeNotebook.id}/documents`);
      if (docRes.ok) {
        const newDocs = await docRes.json();
        setDocuments(newDocs);
        setSelectedDocs(prev => new Set([...prev, data.filename || tempTitle]));
      }
    } catch (e) {
      alert("Lỗi khi nạp văn bản: " + e.message);
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
      setUploadingDocs(prev => prev.filter(d => d.filename !== tempTitle));
    }
  };

  const deleteDocument = async (filename) => {
    if (!activeNotebook) return;
    if (!window.confirm(`Bạn có chắc muốn xóa tài liệu "${filename}"?`)) return;
    
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/notebooks/${activeNotebook.id}/documents?filename=${encodeURIComponent(filename)}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
      if (res.ok) {
        setDocuments(prev => prev.filter(d => d.filename !== filename));
        setSelectedDocs(prev => {
          const next = new Set(prev);
          next.delete(filename);
          return next;
        });
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Error deleting document:', err);
    }
  };

  const handleSend = async () => {
    if (!input.trim() || !activeNotebook) return;
    
    // Hủy bỏ request trước đó nếu còn đang chạy
    if (abortControllerRef.current) {
      abortControllerRef.current.abort();
    }
    abortControllerRef.current = new AbortController();

    const userMsg = input.trim();
    setInput('');
    setMessages(prev => [...prev, { role: 'user', content: userMsg }]);
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      setMessages(prev => [...prev, { role: 'assistant', content: '', citations: [] }]);
      
      const response = await fetch('/api/chat', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          'x-notebook-id': activeNotebook.id
        },
        signal: abortControllerRef.current.signal,
        body: JSON.stringify({ 
          query: userMsg, 
          persona: chatPersona,
          notebook_id: activeNotebook.id,
          provider: activeNotebook.provider,
          model_name: activeNotebook.model_name,
          selected_documents: Array.from(selectedDocs)
        })
      });

      const reader = response.body.getReader();
      const decoder = new TextDecoder("utf-8");
      
      let buffer = "";

      while (true) {
        const { value, done } = await reader.read();
        if (done) break;
        
        buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
        
        const events = buffer.split('\n\n');
        buffer = events.pop() || "";
        
        for (const event of events) {
          if (event.startsWith("data: ")) {
            const dataStr = event.substring(6);
            try {
              const data = JSON.parse(dataStr);
              
              setMessages(prev => {
                const newMessages = [...prev];
                const lastIndex = newMessages.length - 1;
                
                if (data.type === 'citations') {
                  newMessages[lastIndex] = {
                    ...newMessages[lastIndex],
                    citations: data.citations
                  };
                } else if (data.type === 'chunk') {
                  newMessages[lastIndex] = {
                    ...newMessages[lastIndex],
                    content: newMessages[lastIndex].content + data.text,
                    status: null
                  };
                } else if (data.type === 'status') {
                  newMessages[lastIndex] = {
                    ...newMessages[lastIndex],
                    status: data.message
                  };
                } else if (data.type === 'saved') {
                  if (lastIndex >= 1 && data.user_msg_id) {
                    newMessages[lastIndex - 1] = { ...newMessages[lastIndex - 1], id: data.user_msg_id };
                  }
                  if (data.assistant_msg_id) {
                    newMessages[lastIndex] = { ...newMessages[lastIndex], id: data.assistant_msg_id };
                  }
                }
                
                return newMessages;
              });
            } catch (e) {
              console.error("Lỗi parse JSON SSE", e);
            }
          }
        }
      }
    } catch (err) {
      if (err.name === 'AbortError') {
        console.log("Đã dừng tạo phản hồi.");
        return;
      }
      console.error(err);
      setMessages(prev => {
        const newMessages = [...prev];
        const lastIndex = newMessages.length - 1;
        newMessages[lastIndex] = {
          ...newMessages[lastIndex],
          content: 'Xin lỗi, có lỗi xảy ra khi kết nối tới LLM Engine.'
        };
        return newMessages;
      });
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
      abortControllerRef.current = null;
    }
  };

  // Tính năng: Dừng câu trả lời ngay lập tức
  const handleStopGenerating = () => {
    if (abortControllerRef.current) {
      abortControllerRef.current.abort();
      abortControllerRef.current = null;
    }
    setLoading(false);
    setMessages(prev => {
      if (prev.length === 0) return prev;
      const lastIndex = prev.length - 1;
      const lastMsg = prev[lastIndex];
      if (lastMsg && lastMsg.role === 'assistant') {
        const newMessages = [...prev];
        newMessages[lastIndex] = {
          ...lastMsg,
          content: (lastMsg.content || '') + (lastMsg.content ? ' _(Đã dừng câu trả lời)_' : '_(Đã dừng câu trả lời)_'),
          status: null
        };
        return newMessages;
      }
      return prev;
    });
  };

  const handleEvaluate = async () => {
    if (!activeNotebook) return;
    setEvaluating(true);
    try {
      const formData = new FormData();
      formData.append('notebook_id', activeNotebook.id);
      const res = await fetch('/api/evaluate', {
        method: 'POST',
        body: formData
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.status === 'success' && data.metrics) {
        setEvalResult(data.metrics);
        setShowEvalModal(true);
      } else {
        alert("Lỗi đánh giá: " + (data.message || 'Không có kết quả'));
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.error(e);
      alert("Lỗi đánh giá: " + e.message);
    } finally {
      setEvaluating(false);
    }
  };

  const handleKeyDown = (e) => {
    if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) {
      e.preventDefault();
      if (!loading && input.trim()) {
        handleSend();
      }
    }
  };

  if (!activeNotebook) {
    return (
      <div className="h-screen w-full flex items-center justify-center bg-[#131314] text-white">
        <Loader className="animate-spin text-blue-500" size={32} />
      </div>
    );
  }

  const currentProvider = activeNotebook.provider || (activeNotebook.is_private ? 'ollama' : 'gemini');
  const currentModel = activeNotebook.model_name || (currentProvider === 'ollama' ? 'qwen2.5:3b' : 'gemini-2.5-flash');

  return (
    <div className={`h-screen w-full flex overflow-hidden font-sans transition-colors ${
      isDark ? 'bg-[#101216] text-white selection:bg-blue-500/30' : 'bg-[#f8fafc] text-slate-900 selection:bg-blue-200'
    }`}>
      
      {/* Cột 1: Left Sidebar (Sources) */}
      <div className={`${
        showLeftSidebar ? 'w-64 xl:w-72 flex' : 'hidden'
      } flex-shrink-0 h-full border-r flex-col p-4 z-20 relative transition-all duration-200 ${
        isDark ? 'bg-[#14161a] border-white/5' : 'bg-white border-slate-200'
